TL;DR: 本文介绍了GaelEval，首个针对苏格兰盖尔语的多维度评估基准，包含形态句法MCQA、文化翻译基准和文化知识Q&A三个任务。评估19个LLM后发现Gemini 3 Pro Preview在语言任务上超越人类基线(83.3% vs 78.1%)，专有模型优于开源模型，盖尔语提示带来+2.4%提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言LLM在非官方支持的语言中表现出"隐性"能力，但对于形态句法丰富的少数语言（如苏格兰盖尔语），现有翻译基准无法有效捕捉结构性能力，导致评估不充分。

Result: Gemini 3 Pro Preview在语言任务达83.3%准确率（超人类基线78.1%）；专有模型一致优于开源模型；盖尔语提示带来+2.4%稳定优势；文化任务上模型准确率超90%，但盖尔语提示下多数模型表现反而下降。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (LLMs) often exhibit emergent 'shadow' capabilities in languages without official support, yet their performance on these languages remains uneven and under-measured. This is particularly acute for morphosyntactically rich minority languages such as Scottish Gaelic, where translation benchmarks fail to capture structural competence. We introduce GaelEval, the first multi-dimensional benchmark for Gaelic, comprising: (i) an expert-authored morphosyntactic MCQA task; (ii) a culturally grounded translation benchmark and (iii) a large-scale cultural knowledge Q&A task. Evaluating 19 LLMs against a fluent-speaker human baseline ($n=30$), we find that Gemini 3 Pro Preview achieves $83.3\%$ accuracy on the linguistic task, surpassing the human baseline ($78.1\%$). Proprietary models consistently outperform open-weight systems, and in-language (Gaelic) prompting yields a small but stable advantage (+$2.4\%$). On the cultural task, leading models exceed $90\%$ accuracy, though most systems perform worse under Gaelic prompting and absolute scores are inflated relative to the manual benchmark. Overall, GaelEval reveals that frontier models achieve above-human performance on several dimensions of Gaelic grammar, demonstrates the effect of Gaelic prompting and shows a consistent performance gap favouring proprietary over open-weight models.

</details>


### [44] [Brief Is Better: Non-Monotonic Chain-of-Thought Budget Effects in Function-Calling Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02155)
*Xuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了思维链（CoT）推理长度对函数调用智能体性能的影响，发现了一个显著的非单调关系：简短推理（32 tokens）将准确率从44%提升至64%，而超长推理（256 tokens）反而降至25%以下。基于这一发现，提出了结构化的FR-CoT方法，通过模板化推理格式实现零幻觉的函数路由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究对CoT推理长度与准确率之间的关系缺乏系统理解，尤其是在结构化工具调用场景中，推理长度对性能的影响机制尚不明确。

Method: 在Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard v3 Multiple基准的200个任务上，对六个token预算（0-512）进行系统实验，并进行三层误差分解分析以及神谕分析。

Result: Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct模型上呈现非单调性能曲线：32 tokens时准确率峰值达64%（相对提升45%），256 tokens时跌至25%；误差分解揭示简短CoT将函数选择错误从30.5%降至1.5%，而长CoT导致28%选择错误和18%幻觉函数；神谕分析表明88.6%可解任务仅需≤32 tokens。

Conclusion: 简短CoT主要充当函数路由机制，真正的最优推理长度为8-16 tokens；提出的FR-CoT方法通过强制结构化推理格式（"Function: [name] / Key args: [...]"）实现零幻觉，在保持准确率的同时提供了结构可靠性保证。

Abstract: How much should a language agent think before taking action? Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is widely assumed to improve agent performance, but the relationship between reasoning length and accuracy in structured tool-use settings remains poorly understood. We present a systematic study of CoT budget effects on function-calling agents, sweeping six token budgets (0--512) across 200 tasks from the Berkeley Function Calling Leaderboard v3 Multiple benchmark. Our central finding is a striking non-monotonic pattern on Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct: brief reasoning (32 tokens) dramatically improves accuracy by 45% relative over direct answers, from 44.0% to 64.0%, while extended reasoning (256 tokens) degrades performance well below the no-CoT baseline, to 25.0% (McNemar p < 0.001). A three-way error decomposition reveals the mechanism. At d = 0, 30.5% of tasks fail because the model selects the wrong function from the candidate set; brief CoT reduces this to 1.5%, effectively acting as a function-routing step, while long CoT reverses the gain, yielding 28.0% wrong selections and 18.0% hallucinated functions at d = 256. Oracle analysis shows that 88.6% of solvable tasks require at most 32 reasoning tokens, with an average of 27.6 tokens, and a finer-grained sweep indicates that the true optimum lies at 8--16 tokens. Motivated by this routing effect, we propose Function-Routing CoT (FR-CoT), a structured brief-CoT method that templates the reasoning phase as "Function: [name] / Key args: [...]," forcing commitment to a valid function name at the start of reasoning. FR-CoT achieves accuracy statistically equivalent to free-form d = 32 CoT while reducing function hallucination to 0.0%, providing a structural reliability guarantee without budget tuning.

</details>


### [45] [Do Lexical and Contextual Coreference Resolution Systems Degrade Differently under Mention Noise? An Empirical Study on Scientific Software Mentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02171)
*Atilla Kaan Alkan,Felix Grezes,Jennifer Lynn Bartlett,Anna Kelbert,Kelly Lockhart,Alberto Accomazzi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了SOMD 2026跨文档软件提及共指消解共享任务中排名第二的系统，对比了两种无需微调的方法：模糊匹配(FM)和上下文感知表示(CAR)。CAR通过结合提及级和文档级嵌入，在官方测试集上始终比FM高出约1个F1点，两者在CoNLL F1上均达到0.94-0.96的竞争力表现。噪声注入实验揭示了两者的互补失败模式：CAR对边界噪声更鲁棒（仅下降0.07 F1），FM在提及替换下更稳定（下降0.52 vs 0.63）。CAR推理时间随语料规模近似线性增长，FM呈超线性增长，提示系统选择应考虑上游提及检测器的噪声特征和目标语料规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨文档软件提及共指消解是一个尚未被充分探索的任务。本文旨在比较两种无需复杂微调的实用方法，评估其在不同噪声条件和规模下的表现差异，为实际应用中的系统选择提供依据。

Method: 采用两种无需微调的方法：(1) 模糊匹配(FM)，基于词汇字符串相似度；(2) 上下文感知表示(CAR)，结合提及级和文档级嵌入。同时设计了受控噪声注入实验，测试边界噪声和提及替换两种干扰类型。

Result: 两种方法均达到CoNLL F1 0.94-0.96的竞争力性能。CAR在官方测试集上始终比FM高出约1个F1点。噪声实验显示：边界噪声下CAR更鲁棒（FM下降0.20 F1，CAR仅下降0.07）；提及替换下FM更稳定（FM下降0.52，CAR下降0.63）。扩展性方面，FM随语料规模超线性增长，CAR近似线性增长。

Conclusion: 软件名称的高表面规律性降低了对复杂语义推理的需求，使两种简单方法均表现优异。系统选择应根据上游提及检测器的噪声特征和目标语料规模综合考量：大规模场景推荐CAR，小规模或噪声分布不均场景可考虑FM。

Abstract: We present our participation in the SOMD 2026 shared task on cross-document software mention coreference resolution, where our systems ranked second across all three subtasks. We compare two fine-tuning-free approaches: Fuzzy Matching (FM), a lexical string-similarity method, and Context Aware Representations (CAR), which combines mention-level and document-level embeddings. Both achieve competitive performance across all subtasks (CoNLL F1 of 0.94-0.96), with CAR consistently outperforming FM by 1 point on the official test set, consistent with the high surface regularity of software names, which reduces the need for complex semantic reasoning. A controlled noise-injection study reveals complementary failure modes: as boundary noise increases, CAR loses only 0.07 F1 points from clean to fully corrupted input, compared to 0.20 for FM, whereas under mention substitution, FM degrades more gracefully (0.52 vs. 0.63). Our inference-time analysis shows that FM scales superlinearly with corpus size, whereas CAR scales approximately linearly, making CAR the more efficient choice at large scale. These findings suggest that system selection should be informed by both the noise profile of the upstream mention detector and the scale of the target corpus. We release our code to support future work on this underexplored task.

</details>


### [46] [Adam's Law: Textual Frequency Law on Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02176)
*Hongyuan Adam Lu,Z. L.,Victor Wei,Zefan Zhang,Zhao Hong,Qiqi Xiang,Bowen Cao,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将文本频率（Textual Frequency）作为研究LLM的新方向，构建了包含文本频率定律（TFL）、文本频率蒸馏（TFD）和课程文本频率训练（CTFT）的完整框架，通过利用在线资源估计句子级频率，并采用输入改写和课程微调策略提升LLM性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本频率已被证实与人类阅读认知相关（阅读速度），但其与LLM的关系尚未被充分研究。本文旨在探索这一空白领域。

Method: 框架包含三个核心模块：1）TFL：假设频繁文本数据更适合LLM的提示和微调，使用在线资源估计句子级频率，并利用输入改写器将输入转换为更频繁的表达；2）TFD：利用LLM扩展数据集句子以调整初始频率估计；3）CTFT：按句子频率递增顺序对LLM进行课程微调。

Result: 在TFPD数据集上的数学推理、机器翻译、常识推理和智能体工具调用任务中验证了框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 文本频率是一个有价值的研究方向，通过频率感知的提示改写和课程微调策略可有效提升LLM在多任务上的表现。

Abstract: While textual frequency has been validated as relevant to human cognition in reading speed, its relatedness to Large Language Models (LLMs) is seldom studied. We propose a novel research direction in terms of textual data frequency, which is an understudied topic, to the best of our knowledge. Our framework is composed of three units. First, this paper proposes Textual Frequency Law (TFL), which indicates that frequent textual data should be preferred for LLMs for both prompting and fine-tuning. Since many LLMs are closed-source in their training data, we propose using online resources to estimate the sentence-level frequency. We then utilize an input paraphraser to paraphrase the input into a more frequent textual expression. Next, we propose Textual Frequency Distillation (TFD) by querying LLMs to conduct story completion by further extending the sentences in the datasets, and the resulting corpora are used to adjust the initial estimation. Finally, we propose Curriculum Textual Frequency Training (CTFT) that fine-tunes LLMs in an increasing order of sentence-level frequency. Experiments are conducted on our curated dataset Textual Frequency Paired Dataset (TFPD) on math reasoning, machine translation, commonsense reasoning and agentic tool calling. Results show the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [47] [The Expert Strikes Back: Interpreting Mixture-of-Experts Language Models at Expert Level](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02178)
*Jeremy Herbst,Jae Hee Lee,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了MoE架构相比Dense FFN的可解释性问题。通过k-sparse probing方法，发现专家神经元的多义性显著低于Dense FFN神经元，且稀疏性越高差距越大。这表明稀疏性促使神经元和整个专家向单义性演化。基于此，作者将分析单元从神经元提升到专家层面，成功自动解释了大量专家的功能性质，解决了关于专家是否是大规模领域专家的争议——专家实际上是细粒度的任务专家，专注于语言操作或语义任务（如LaTeX括号闭合）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型中广泛采用MoE架构，但其稀疏性是否使其比Dense FFN更易解释尚不明确。研究者希望探究MoE的可解释性特征，并解决"专家究竟是大规模领域专家还是简单token处理器"的争议。

Method: 使用k-sparse probing方法对MoE专家和Dense FFN进行对比分析，并将分析单元从神经元层级提升到专家层级，通过自动解释方法分析数百个专家的功能。

Result: 专家神经元始终比Dense FFN神经元具有更低的多义性，且稀疏程度越高差异越大。专家既不是广泛领域专家（如生物学），也不是简单的token级处理器，而是细粒度的任务专家，专注于特定语言操作或语义任务（如LaTeX中闭合括号）。

Conclusion: MoE在专家层级具有固有的可解释性优势，为大规模模型的可解释性研究提供了更清晰的路径。稀疏性驱动了单义性的演化，使专家成为理解LLM内部工作机制的有效分析单元。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures have become the dominant choice for scaling Large Language Models (LLMs), activating only a subset of parameters per token. While MoE architectures are primarily adopted for computational efficiency, it remains an open question whether their sparsity makes them inherently easier to interpret than dense feed-forward networks (FFNs). We compare MoE experts and dense FFNs using $k$-sparse probing and find that expert neurons are consistently less polysemantic, with the gap widening as routing becomes sparser. This suggests that sparsity pressures both individual neurons and entire experts toward monosemanticity. Leveraging this finding, we zoom out from the neuron to the expert level as a more effective unit of analysis. We validate this approach by automatically interpreting hundreds of experts. This analysis allows us to resolve the debate on specialization: experts are neither broad domain specialists (e.g., biology) nor simple token-level processors. Instead, they function as fine-grained task experts, specializing in linguistic operations or semantic tasks (e.g., closing brackets in LaTeX). Our findings suggest that MoEs are inherently interpretable at the expert level, providing a clearer path toward large-scale model interpretability. Code is available at: https://github.com/jerryy33/MoE_analysis

</details>


### [48] [Neuro-RIT: Neuron-Guided Instruction Tuning for Robust Retrieval-Augmented Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02194)
*Jaemin Kim,Jae O Lee,Sumyeong Ahn,Seo Yeon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Neuro-RIT通过神经元级精细调控提升检索增强语言模型对噪声上下文的鲁棒性，通过属性溯源挖掘区分相关/无关上下文的神经元，并采用双阶段指令微调策略实现噪声抑制与证据蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RALM在处理不相关或噪声检索上下文时性能下降严重；现有增强鲁棒性的方法采用粗粒度的层或模块级参数更新，忽视了LLM固有的神经元级稀疏性这一重要特性。

Method: 提出Neuro-RIT框架，核心包括：(1)基于属性溯源的神经元挖掘，明确分离处理相关上下文与无关上下文的神经元；(2)两阶段指令微调策略——通过功能性地失活专属于无关上下文的神经元实现直接噪声抑制，同时优化目标层进行证据蒸馏。

Result: 在多个QA基准数据集上的实验表明，Neuro-RIT持续优于强基线和现有鲁棒性增强方法。

Conclusion: 该研究将LLM适应范式从密集参数调整转向精准神经元对齐，验证了神经元级稀疏性在提升RAG系统鲁棒性中的关键作用。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Language Models (RALMs) have demonstrated significant potential in knowledge-intensive tasks; however, they remain vulnerable to performance degradation when presented with irrelevant or noisy retrieved contexts. Existing approaches to enhance robustness typically operate via coarse-grained parameter updates at the layer or module level, often overlooking the inherent neuron-level sparsity of Large Language Models (LLMs). To address this limitation, we propose Neuro-RIT (Neuron-guided Robust Instruction Tuning), a novel framework that shifts the paradigm from dense adaptation to precision-driven neuron alignment. Our method explicitly disentangles neurons that are responsible for processing relevant versus irrelevant contexts using attribution-based neuron mining. Subsequently, we introduce a two-stage instruction tuning strategy that enforces a dual capability for noise robustness: achieving direct noise suppression by functionally deactivating neurons exclusive to irrelevant contexts, while simultaneously optimizing targeted layers for evidence distillation. Extensive experiments across diverse QA benchmarks demonstrate that Neuro-RIT consistently outperforms strong baselines and robustness-enhancing methods.

</details>


### [49] [CV-18 NER: Augmented Common Voice for Named Entity Recognition from Arabic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02209)
*Youssef Saidi,Haroun Elleuch,Fethi Bougares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个面向阿拉伯语语音的端到端命名实体识别（NER）基准数据集CV-18 NER，结合Wojood实体模式（21类实体），对比了流水线系统与端到端模型。端到端模型显著优于最佳流水线配置，Whisper-medium达到38.0% CVER，AraBEST-RQ 300M达到37.0% CoER。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿拉伯语因其形态复杂性、元音省略及标注资源匮乏，在端到端语音NER任务中尚未得到充分探索。现有级联流水线（ASR+NLI）存在错误传播问题，缺乏统一的语音到实体的联合学习基准。

Method: 在Arabic Common Voice 18语料库基础上人工标注NER标签，构建CV-18 NER数据集；对比测试了流水线系统（ASR+文本NER）与基于Whisper和AraBEST-RQ的端到端模型。

Result: 端到端模型显著优于流水线系统：AraBEST-RQ 300M达到37.0% CoER，Whisper-medium达到38.0% CVER。阿拉伯语自监督预训练有利于ASR性能；多语言弱监督信号更有效迁移到语音-实体联合学习；低资源场景下大模型适配更难。

Conclusion: 端到端架构是阿拉伯语语音NER的有效方案，多语言弱监督学习策略具有优势，数据集和模型已开源发布，为该领域首个公开基准。

Abstract: End-to-end speech Named Entity Recognition (NER) aims to directly extract entities from speech. Prior work has shown that end-to-end (E2E) approaches can outperform cascaded pipelines for English, French, and Chinese, but Arabic remains under-explored due to its morphological complexity, the absence of short vowels, and limited annotated resources. We introduce CV-18 NER, the first publicly available dataset for NER from Arabic speech, created by augmenting the Arabic Common Voice 18 corpus with manual NER annotations following the fine-grained Wojood schema (21 entity types). We benchmark both pipeline systems (ASR + text NER) and E2E models based on Whisper and AraBEST-RQ. E2E systems substantially outperform the best pipeline configuration on the test set, reaching 37.0% CoER (AraBEST-RQ 300M) and 38.0% CVER (Whisper-medium). Further analysis shows that Arabic-specific self-supervised pretraining yields strong ASR performance, while multilingual weak supervision transfers more effectively to joint speech-to-entity learning, and that larger models may be harder to adapt in this low-resource setting. Our dataset and models are publicly released, providing the first open benchmark for end-to-end named entity recognition from Arabic speech https://huggingface.co/datasets/Elyadata/CV18-NER.

</details>


### [50] [No Single Best Model for Diversity: Learning a Router for Sample Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02319)
*Yuhan Liu,Fangyuan Xu,Vishakh Padmakumar,Daphne Ippolito,Eunsol Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出使用"多样性覆盖率"评估LLM生成多样化响应的能力，并设计了一个路由器模型来为不同查询选择最优模型，在NB-Wildchat数据集上实现了26.3% vs 23.8%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当面对允许大量有效答案的开放性问题时，如何全面生成多样化的有效响应是一个重要挑战。现有研究缺乏对模型生成多样化答案能力的系统性评估，以及如何在不同模型间做出最优选择的解决方案。

Method: 作者提出"多样性覆盖率"(diversity coverage)指标，评估18个LLM生成多样化答案的能力。通过分析发现单个模型难以在所有提示上表现最优，因此设计了路由器模型，根据查询预测最佳模型。实验在NB-Wildchat和NB-Curated数据集上进行测试。

Result: 评估显示没有单一模型能在所有开放性提示上占据主导地位，但对每个提示都存在表现显著优于其他模型的模型。路由器在NB-Wildchat上达到26.3%的多样性覆盖率，优于最佳单一模型基线的23.8%，并具有良好的跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 研究成果为在多模型环境下研究全面答案生成奠定了基础。路由器方法证明了针对不同查询选择特定模型的有效性，为构建更强大的多样化响应生成系统提供了新思路。

Abstract: When posed with prompts that permit a large number of valid answers, comprehensively generating them is the first step towards satisfying a wide range of users. In this paper, we study methods to elicit a comprehensive set of valid responses. To evaluate this, we introduce \textbf{diversity coverage}, a metric that measures the total quality scores assigned to each \textbf{unique} answer in the predicted answer set relative to the best possible answer set with the same number of answers. Using this metric, we evaluate 18 LLMs, finding no single model dominates at generating diverse responses to a wide range of open-ended prompts. Yet, per each prompt, there exists a model that outperforms all other models significantly at generating a diverse answer set. Motivated by this finding, we introduce a router that predicts the best model for each query. On NB-Wildchat, our trained router outperforms the single best model baseline (26.3% vs $23.8%). We further show generalization to an out-of-domain dataset (NB-Curated) as well as different answer-generation prompting strategies. Our work lays foundation for studying generating comprehensive answers when we have access to a suite of models.

</details>


### [51] [Grounded Token Initialization for New Vocabulary in LMs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02324)
*Daiwei Chen,Zhoutong Fu,Chengming Jiang,Haichao Zhang,Ran Zhou,Tan Wang,Chunnan Yao,Guoyao Li,Rui Cai,Yihan Cao,Ruijie Jiang,Fedor Borisyuk,Jianqiang Shen,Jingwei Wu,Ramya Korlakai Vinayak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了语言模型扩展新词汇时，传统的均值初始化策略会导致新token退化为同一子空间，抹杀了token间的区分性。为解决此问题，提出了GTI方法，通过在微调前将新token映射到预训练嵌入空间的语义有意义位置来改进初始化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型扩展新词汇的标准做法是将新token初始化为现有词嵌入的均值，然后通过监督微调学习表示。但作者发现这种均值初始化会导致所有新token坍缩到同一退化子空间，微调难以完全恢复token间的区分性，成为词汇扩展的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出Grounded Token Initialization Hypothesis：主张在微调前通过语言学监督将新token锚定到预训练嵌入空间的语义位置。操作化为GTI方法：首先使用配对的语言学监督信号，将新token映射到预训练嵌入空间中不同且语义有意义的位置，然后进行标准微调。

Result: 在多个生成式推荐基准测试（包括工业级和公开数据集）上，GTI在大多数评估设置中优于均值初始化和现有辅助任务适应方法。进一步分析表明，锚定的嵌入产生更丰富的token间结构，且该结构在微调后得以保持。

Conclusion: 初始化质量是词汇扩展的关键瓶颈。通过在微调前将新token锚定到预训练嵌入空间的语义位置，可以更好地利用模型的通用知识来适应新领域。GTI是一个轻量级且简单的方法，能有效解决token初始化问题。

Abstract: Language models (LMs) are increasingly extended with new learnable vocabulary tokens for domain-specific tasks, such as Semantic-ID tokens in generative recommendation. The standard practice initializes these new tokens as the mean of existing vocabulary embeddings, then relies on supervised fine-tuning to learn their representations. We present a systematic analysis of this strategy: through spectral and geometric diagnostics, we show that mean initialization collapses all new tokens into a degenerate subspace, erasing inter-token distinctions that subsequent fine-tuning struggles to fully recover. These findings suggest that \emph{token initialization} is a key bottleneck when extending LMs with new vocabularies. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose the \emph{Grounded Token Initialization Hypothesis}: linguistically grounding novel tokens in the pretrained embedding space before fine-tuning better enables the model to leverage its general-purpose knowledge for novel-token domains. We operationalize this hypothesis as GTI (Grounded Token Initialization), a lightweight grounding stage that, prior to fine-tuning, maps new tokens to distinct, semantically meaningful locations in the pretrained embedding space using only paired linguistic supervision. Despite its simplicity, GTI outperforms both mean initialization and existing auxiliary-task adaptation methods in the majority of evaluation settings across multiple generative recommendation benchmarks, including industry-scale and public datasets. Further analyses show that grounded embeddings produce richer inter-token structure that persists through fine-tuning, corroborating the hypothesis that initialization quality is a key bottleneck in vocabulary extension.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [52] [EXaCTz: Guaranteed Extremum Graph and Contour Tree Preservation for Distributed- and GPU-Parallel Lossy Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01397)
*Yuxiao Li,Mingze Xia,Xin Liang,Bei Wang,Hanqi Guo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: EXaCTz是一种并行算法，在有损压缩的标量场数据中同时保持极值图和轮廓树的拓扑一致性。该算法解决了现有拓扑保持方法存在的吞吐量差距大、拓扑描述符支持有限、缺乏理论收敛保证等问题，通过强制执行所有顶点的最小/最大邻居一致性以及关键点间的全局排序，实现了一致的临界点分类、鞍点-极值连通性以及分裂/合并事件的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大尺度科学模拟和流工作流中，误差有界有损压缩至关重要，但现有拓扑保持方法存在三个主要问题：(1) 严重的吞吐量差距——拓扑校正速度仅为MB/s量级，而压缩速度可达GB/s量级；(2) 对多样化拓扑描述符的支持有限；(3) 缺乏理论收敛边界。这些问题限制了拓扑保持压缩在实际应用中的效率。

Method: EXaCTz提出了一种高性能、有界迭代算法，通过对解压缩数据推导针对性编辑来强制拓扑一致性。与显式拓扑重建方法不同，该算法通过强制所有顶点的最小/最大邻居一致性、关键点间的全局排序来实现：(1) 一致的临界点分类；(2) 鞍点-极值连通性保持；(3) 分裂/合并事件保持。算法收敛性被理论证明，收敛上界由脆弱性图中的最长路径刻画。

Result: 单GPU吞吐量达4.52 GB/s，比Gorski等人提出的最新轮廓树保持方法快213倍（单核CPU公平比较）或3285倍（单GPU版本）。分布式环境下可扩展至128个GPU，效率达55.6%（vs朴素并行化的6.4%），在48秒内处理512GB数据集，聚合校正吞吐量达32.69 GB/s。

Conclusion: EXaCTz通过有界迭代和针对性编辑策略，成功解决了拓扑保持压缩中的性能瓶颈问题。理论收敛性保证和优异的大规模实验结果表明，该方法在大规模科学数据处理中具有显著优势，为实时或准实时的拓扑感知压缩应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces EXaCTz, a parallel algorithm that concurrently preserves extremum graphs and contour trees in lossy-compressed scalar field data. While error-bounded lossy compression is essential for large-scale scientific simulations and workflows, existing topology-preserving methods suffer from (1) a significant throughput disparity, where topology correction speeds are on the order of MB/s, lagging orders of magnitude behind compression speeds on the order of GB/s, (2) limited support for diverse topological descriptors, and (3) a lack of theoretical convergence bounds. To address these challenges, EXaCTz introduces a high-performance, bounded-iteration algorithm that enforces topological consistency by deriving targeted edits for decompressed data. Unlike prior methods that rely on explicit topology reconstruction, EXaCTz enforces consistent min/max neighbors of all vertices, along with global ordering among critical points. As such, the algorithm enforces consistent critical-point classification, saddle extremum connectivity, and the preservation of merge/split events. We theoretically prove the convergence of our algorithm, bounded by the longest path in a vulnerability graph that characterizes potential cascading effects during correction. Experiments on real-world datasets show that EXaCTz achieves a single-GPU throughput of up to 4.52 GB/s, outperforming the state-of-the-art contour-tree-preserving method (Gorski et al.) by up to 213x (with a single-core CPU implementation for fair comparison) and 3,285x (with a single-GPU version). In distributed environments, EXaCTz scales to 128 GPUs with 55.6\% efficiency (compared with 6.4\% for a naive parallelization), processing datasets of up to 512 GB in under 48 seconds and achieving an aggregate correction throughput of up to 32.69 GB/s.

</details>


### [53] [ModTrans: Translating Real-world Models for Distributed Training Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01607)
*Yi Lyu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ModTrans是一个翻译器，可将任何真实世界的模型格式转换为ASTRA-sim模拟器的输入格式，解决现有分布式训练模拟器无法导入真实模型的问题，使机器学习专家能够便捷地利用模拟器进行实验研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模分布式训练是机器学习系统领域的热点研究问题，但缺乏物理机器和资源时难以进行实验验证。虽然ASTRA-sim等分布式训练模拟器提供了解决方案，但其不支持导入真实世界开发的模型，导致机器学习研究人员难以使用这些模拟器，形成了ML专家与ML系统研究者之间的壁垒。

Method: 开发了ModTrans翻译器，支持从任意真实世界模型到ASTRA-sim模拟器输入格式的格式转换，从而实现模型的无缝导入与模拟。

Result: 实验结果表明，ModTrans的转换开销可以忽略不计。

Conclusion: ModTrans有效消除了机器学习专家与机器学习系统研究者之间的技术壁垒，使得ML专家能够便捷地利用ASTRA-sim等模拟器进行分布式训练相关的实验研究。

Abstract: Large-scale distributed training has been a research hot spot in machine learning systems for industry and academia in recent years. However, conducting experiments without physical machines and corresponding resources is difficult. One solution is to leverage distributed training simulators, but current ones like ASTRA-sim do not support importing real-world developed models, which poses challenges for ML researchers seeking to use them. Based on this challenge, we developed ModTrans, a translator supporting format translation from any real-world model to the ASTRA-sim simulator's input, removing the barrier between machine learning experts and machine learning system researchers. The experiment results show that ModTrans's cost is negligible.

</details>


### [54] [DWDP: Distributed Weight Data Parallelism for High-Performance LLM Inference on NVL72](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01621)
*Wanqian Li,Jintao Peng,Zongfei Jing,Tianyu Zhang,Ze Long,Xianjie Qiao,Xiaoming Chen,Dongxu Yang,Kefeng Duan,June Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DWDP是一种用于大型语言模型多GPU推理的并行化策略，通过将MoE权重卸载到对等GPU并按需获取缺失的专家，同时移除GPU间的集合同步，使每个GPU能够独立运行。结合TensorRT-LLM实现并在GB200 NVL72上测试DeepSeek-R1，在8K输入/1K输出序列长度下，TPS/GPU提升8.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理的多GPU并行化策略需要层间GPU间同步，导致端到端性能对工作负载不平衡敏感，限制了推理效率的提升。

Method: 提出DWDP（分布式权重数据并行），将MoE权重分片卸载到各对等GPU，按需获取缺失专家，同时移除集合式GPU间同步，使各GPU独立推进；并设计分片权重管理和异步远程权重预取两项优化。

Result: 在GB200 NVL72上使用DeepSeek-R1测试，8K输入序列和1K输出序列长度下，在20-100 TPS/user服务范围内，TPS/GPU提升8.8%，且TPS/user保持相当水平。

Conclusion: DWDP通过消除同步开销和优化权重管理，能在保持数据并行执行优势的同时，有效提升多GPU推理的端到端吞吐量。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) inference increasingly depends on multi-GPU execution, yet existing inference parallelization strategies require layer-wise inter-rank synchronization, making end-to-end performance sensitive to workload imbalance. We present DWDP (Distributed Weight Data Parallelism), an inference parallelization strategy that preserves data-parallel execution while offloading MoE weights across peer GPUs and fetching missing experts on demand. By removing collective inter-rank synchronization, DWDP allows each GPU to progress independently. We further address the practical overheads of this design with two optimizations for split-weight management and asynchronous remote-weight prefetch. Implemented in TensorRT-LLM and evaluated with DeepSeek-R1 on GB200 NVL72, DWDP improves end-to-end output TPS/GPU by 8.8% at comparable TPS/user in the 20-100 TPS/user serving range under 8K input sequence length and 1K output sequence length.

</details>


### [55] [Optimization Opportunities for Cloud-Based Data Pipeline Infrastructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01954)
*Johannes Jablonski,Georg-Daniel Schwarz,Philip Heltweg,Dirk Riehle*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对云基础设施数据管道的优化方法进行了系统文献综述，提出了包含最小化成本、减少执行时间和成本-完工时间权衡等优化目标的理论框架，涵盖单云与多云、批处理与流处理等维度，并指出了多租户环境研究和行业评估方面的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云基础设施需要优化数据管道的成本、速度和资源利用率，但目前缺乏对云数据管道优化方法的系统性整合理解。

Method: 采用系统文献综述方法，对现有云基础设施数据管道优化相关研究进行系统性梳理和分析。

Result: 提出了一个包含多维度的优化目标理论框架，涵盖成本最小化、执行时间减少、成本-完工时间权衡等目标，以及单云与多云、批处理与流处理等维度。

Conclusion: 指出了当前研究中的空白领域，包括多租户环境探索不足和缺乏行业评估验证，并提出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Cloud infrastructure supports the efficient operation of data pipelines regarding requirements like cost, speed, and resource utilization. We present an integrated view of optimization opportunities for cloud-based data pipelines by conducting a systematic review of existing literature on optimization approaches to cloud infrastructure performance for data pipelines. Our study contributes a theory of optimization goals like minimizing cost, reducing execution time, and cost-makespan trade-offs, consisting of dimensions such as single vs. multi-cloud, batch vs. stream processing, etc. We highlight gaps in primary research, including the underexploration of multi-tenant environments and lack of industry evaluation, and suggest directions for future research.

</details>


### [56] [A Practical Two-Stage Framework for GPU Resource and Power Prediction in Heterogeneous HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02158)
*Beste Oztop,Dhruva Kulkarni,Zhengji Zhao,Ayse Kivilcim Coskun,Kadidia Konate*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对NERSC Perlmutter超级计算机上的VASP材料科学应用，分析GPU利用率、GPU内存利用率和功耗，并提出一个两阶段资源预测框架来预测GPU功耗和利用率，为高效调度和功耗感知系统运维提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU在高性能计算中的需求增长，高效利用GPU资源和功耗管理变得至关重要。VASP是NERSC Perlmutter系统上广泛使用的材料科学应用，但缺乏对其GPU资源利用特征的深入分析。

Method: 使用Slurm工作负载管理器的历史日志和NVIDIA DCGM收集的GPU性能指标进行分析；提出两阶段资源预测框架：第一阶段仅使用Slurm accounting日志作为训练数据，第二阶段结合DCGM历史GPU性能指标增强训练数据。

Result: 仅使用Slurm提交特征的GPU最大利用率预测准确率达97%；从GPU计算和内存活动指标中提取的特征与平均功耗相关性良好，运行时功耗预测准确率达92%。

Conclusion: DCGM指标能有效捕获应用特征，可用于开发预测模型以支持HPC系统的动态功耗管理；Slurm日志特征结合DCGM指标为异构HPC系统的调度优化和功耗感知运维提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Efficient utilization of GPU resources and power has become critical with the growing demand for GPUs in high-performance computing (HPC). In this paper, we analyze GPU utilization and GPU memory utilization, as well as the power consumption of the Vienna ab initio Simulation Package (VASP), using the Slurm workload manager historical logs and GPU performance metrics collected by NVIDIA's Data Center GPU Manager (DCGM). VASP is a widely used materials science application on Perlmutter at NERSC, an HPE Cray EX system based on NVIDIA A100 GPUs. Using our insights from the resource utilization analysis of VASP applications, we propose a resource prediction framework to predict the average GPU power, maximum GPU utilization, and maximum GPU memory utilization values of heterogeneous HPC system applications to enable more efficient scheduling decisions and power-aware system operation. Our prediction framework consists of two stages: 1) using only the Slurm accounting logs as training data and 2) augmenting the training data with historical GPU profiling metrics collected with DCGM. The maximum GPU utilization predictions using only the Slurm submission features achieve up to 97% accuracy. Furthermore, features engineered from GPU-compute and memory activity metrics exhibit good correlations with average power utilization, and our runtime power usage prediction experiments result in up to 92% prediction accuracy. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of DCGM metrics in capturing application characteristics and highlight their potential for developing predictive models to support dynamic power management in HPC systems.

</details>


### [57] [Source Known Identifiers: A Three-Tier Identity System for Distributed Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00151)
*Duran Serkan Kılıç*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Source Known Identifiers (SKIDs)三层标识系统，在单一实体身份跨越信任边界时，同时满足存储效率、时间可排序性、来源元数据嵌入、零查询可验证性、外部机密性及多世纪可寻址性等六项需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标识方案无法同时满足分布式应用对存储效率、时间排序、来源元数据嵌入、零查询验证、外部机密性和长期可寻址性这六项核心需求。

Method: 设计三层标识系统：(1) SKID：64位有符号整数，嵌入250毫秒精度时间戳、应用拓扑信息和实体类型序列计数器，8字节存储，支持B-tree索引自然排序；(2) SKEID：128位UUID兼容值，扩展加入实体类型区分符、历元选择器和BLAKE3 MAC，支持可信环境内零查询验证来源、完整性和实体类型，大端字节序保留UUID字符串字典序排列；(3) Secure SKEID：使用AES-256对称加密SKEID为单块伪随机置换，密文与随机字节不可区分且兼容标准UUID数据解析器。三层间建立确定性双向转换。

Result: SKIDs系统完整满足全部六项标识属性要求。

Conclusion: SKIDs通过三层递进架构，在统一系统内首次实现六项关键标识属性的融合，为分布式应用提供了安全、高效、可验证的标识解决方案。

Abstract: Distributed applications need identifiers that satisfy storage efficiency, chronological sortability, origin metadata embedding, zero-lookup verifiability, confidentiality for external consumers, and multi-century addressability. Based on our literature survey, no existing scheme provides all six of these identifier properties within a unified system.
  This paper introduces Source Known Identifiers (SKIDs), a three-tier identity system that projects a single entity identity across trust boundaries, addressing all six properties. The first tier, Source Known ID (SKID), is a 64-bit signed integer embedding a timestamp with a 250-millisecond precision, application topology, and a per-entity-type sequence counter. It serves as the database primary key, providing compact storage (8 bytes) and natural B-tree ordering for optimized database indexing. The second tier, Source Known Entity ID (SKEID), extends the SKID into a 128-bit Universally Unique Identifier (UUID) compatible value by adding an entity type discriminator, an epoch selector, and a BLAKE3 keyed message authentication code (MAC). SKEIDs enable zero-lookup verification of identifier origin, integrity, and entity type within trusted environments, with a big-endian byte layout that preserves chronological ordering in lexicographic UUID string comparisons. The third tier, Secure SKEID, encrypts the entire SKEID using AES-256 symmetric encryption as a single-block pseudorandom permutation, producing ciphertext indistinguishable from random bytes while remaining compatible with standard UUID data-type parsers in string representation. Deterministic bidirectional transformations connect all three tiers.

</details>

### [58] [Know Your Streams: On the Conceptualization, Characterization, and Generation of Intentional Event Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01440)
*Andrea Maldonado,Christian Imenkamp,Hendrik Reiter,Thomas Seidl,Wilhelm Hasselbring,Martin Werner,Agnes Koschmider*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对当前流式过程挖掘(SPM)算法评估仍依赖过时实践的问题，本文通过系统性文献综述识别了流数据特征，提出扩展事件流概念基础，并开发了Stream of Intent原型生成器，用于生成具有特定特征的可复现事件流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SPM算法评估方法存在明显缺陷：依赖静态日志或人为"流化"的数据，无法真实反映物联网环境下连续实时事件流的复杂性（如乱序事件、并发活动、不完整案例、概念漂移等特征）。

Method: 1) 系统性文献综述，识别当前流数据特征；2) 扩展事件流概念基础；3) 开发Stream of Intent原型生成器

Result: 成功生成可复现的、意图驱动的事件流，适用于有针对性的基准测试和自适应算法开发。

Conclusion: Stream of Intent能有效产生符合真实场景复杂性的事件流，为SPM算法的评估和开发提供了更可靠的基准测试基础。

Abstract: The shift toward IoT-enabled, sensor-driven systems has transformed how operational data is generated, favoring continuous, real-time event streams (ES) over static event logs. This evolution presents new challenges for Streaming Process Mining (SPM), which must cope with out-of-order events, concurrent activities, incomplete cases, and concept drifts. Yet, the evaluation of SPM algorithms remains rooted in outdated practices, relying on static logs or artificially streamified data that fail to reflect the complexities of real-world streams. To address this gap, we first perform a comprehensive review of data stream literature to identify stream characteristics currently not reflected in the SPM community. Next, we use this information to extend the conceptual foundation for ES. Finally, we propose Stream of Intent, a prototype generator to produce ES with specific features. Our evaluation shows excellence in producing reproducible, intentional ES for targeted benchmarking and adaptive algorithm development in SPM.

</details>


### [59] [CogPic: A Multimodal Dataset for Early Cognitive Impairment Assessment via Picture Description Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01626)
*Liuyu Wu,Rui Feng,Jie Li,Wentao Xiang,Yi Zhang,Yin Cao,Siyang Song,Xiao Gu,Jianqing Li,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: CogPic是一个用于认知障碍检测的大规模多模态基准数据库，收集了574名参与者在图片描述任务中的严格同步的音频、视觉和语言数据，并经过专家神经心理学家的临床评估验证，是目前同类中规模最大、模态最丰富、评估最详尽的数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化认知状态评估在早期痴呆诊断中具有重要前景，但认知障碍检测的机器学习模型开发常受限于缺乏大规模、严格同步且经临床验证的多模态数据集。这一关键差距阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 建立CogPic数据库，包含574名参与者在自然图片描述任务中连续收集的严格同步音频、视觉和语言数据；由专家临床神经心理学家进行详尽评估，通过综合临床共识将参与者分层为不同认知组别。

Result: CogPic成为目前同类中规模最大、模态最丰富、评估最详尽的数据集；基于该数据集进行了广泛的基准实验，建立了稳健、无偏且具有临床可推广性的基础。

Conclusion: CogPic数据库为推动自动化认知健康评估的多媒体研究提供了强有力的基础，可用于未来多媒体研究中的认知障碍检测模型训练与验证。

Abstract: The automated evaluation of cognitive status utilizing multimedia technologies presents a promising frontier in early dementia diagnosis. However, the development of robust machine learning models for cognitive impairment detection is frequently hindered by the scarcity of large-scale, strictly synchronized, and clinically validated multimodal datasets. To bridge this critical gap, we introduce the CogPic database, a comprehensive multimodal benchmark meticulously designed for fine-grained cognitive impairment detection. The dataset comprises strictly synchronized audio, visual, and linguistic data continuously collected from 574 participants during a naturalistic picture description task. To establish highly reliable diagnostic ground truth, expert clinical neuropsychologists conducted exhaustive evaluations, stratifying participants into distinct cognitive groups through a comprehensive clinical consensus. Consequently, CogPic stands as the largest, most modality-rich, and most meticulously evaluated dataset of its kind to date. By conducting extensive benchmark experiments on the CogPic dataset, we establish an exceptionally robust, unbiased, and clinically generalizable foundation to propel future multimedia research in automated cognitive health assessment. Detailed information and access application procedures for our CogPic database are available at https://cogpic.github.io/.

</details>


### [60] [GPU-RMQ: Accelerating Range Minimum Queries on Modern GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01811)
*Lara Kreis,Justus Henneberg,Valentin Henkys,Felix Schuhknecht,Bertil Schmidt*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出GPU-RMQ，一种基于层次化方法的新型GPU加速范围最小值查询数据结构，通过在数组上构建多层范围最小摘要，并结合光线追踪核心与CUDA核心的混合设计，显著提升查询吞吐量、降低内存占用、加快索引构建速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GPU-based RMQ方法（如LCA、RTXRMQ）存在三个核心问题：严重的内存开销、高索引构建时间和低查询吞吐量，导致这些方法在大规模数组（≥2^29）上无法在NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU上运行。

Method: GPU-RMQ采用层次化架构：(1) 以高度并行方式在原始数组上构建范围最小摘要层次结构；(2) 查询时仅处理层次结构的相关部分，采用优化的大规模并行扫描操作；(3) 混合设计：不同层次级别协同使用光线追踪核心和CUDA核心处理查询。

Result: 实验结果表明：查询吞吐量方面比LCA提升约8倍，比RTXRMQ提升约17倍，比优化CPU方法提升约4800倍；同时内存占用显著降低，索引构建速度提升最高达两个数量级。

Conclusion: GPU-RMQ在处理大规模数组时具备明显优势，其层次化混合架构设计有效解决了现有方法的内存瓶颈问题，为生物序列分析、文档检索等大规模数据场景提供了高效的RMQ解决方案。

Abstract: Range minimum queries are frequently used in string processing and database applications including biological sequence analysis, document retrieval, and web search. Hence, various data structures have been proposed for improving their efficiency on both CPUs and GPUs.Recent work has also shown that hardware-accelerated ray tracing on modern NVIDIA RTX graphic cards can be exploited to answer range minimum queries by expressing queries as rays, which are fired into a scene of triangles representing minima of ranges at different granularities. While these approaches are promising, they suffer from at least one of three issues: severe memory overhead, high index construction time, and low query throughput. This renders these methods practically unusable on larger arrays: For example, the state-of-art GPU-based approaches LCA and RTXRMQ exceed the memory capacity of an NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU for input arrays of size >= 2^29. To tackle these problems, in this work, we present a new approach called GPU-RMQ which is based on a hierarchical approach. GPU-RMQ first constructs a hierarchy of range minimum summaries on top of the original array in a highly parallel fashion. For query answering, only the relevant portions of the hierarchy are then processed in an optimized massively-parallel scan operation. Additionally, GPU-RMQ is hybrid in design enabling the use of both ray tracing cores and CUDA cores across different levels of the hierarchy to handle queries. Our experimental evaluation shows that GPU-RMQ outperforms the state-of-the-art approaches in terms of query throughput especially for larger arrays while offering a significantly lower memory footprint and up to two orders-of-magnitude faster index construction. In particular, it achieves up to ~8x higher throughput than LCA, ~17x higher throughput than RTXRMQ, and up to ~4800x higher throughput compared to an optimized CPU-based approach.

</details>


### [61] [BBC: Improving Large-k Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search with a Bucket-based Result Collector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01960)
*Ziqi Yin,Gao Cong,Kai Zeng,Jinwei Zhu,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对大k近似最近邻查询性能退化问题，提出了一种新的基于桶的结果收集器（BBC），通过基于桶的结果缓冲区和针对不同量化方法的两种重排序算法来提升性能，在真实数据集上实现最高3.8倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大k近似最近邻（ANN）查询在现实应用中有重要需求，但现有ANN方法在此类查询上性能显著下降。原因包括：(1) 现有top-k收集器效率低下，在候选维护中产生显著开销；(2) 量化方法的剪枝效果降低，导致代价高昂的重排序过程。

Method: 提出基于桶的结果收集器（BBC），包含两个关键组件：(1) 基于桶的结果缓冲区——按候选对象到查询的距离组织成桶，降低排序成本并提高缓存效率，实现高效的候选超集维护和轻量级top-k结果最终选择；(2) 针对不同类型量化方法量身定制的两种重排序算法，通过减少重排序候选对象数量或缓存未命中来加速重排序过程。

Result: 在真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，BBC在recall@k = 0.95的大k ANN查询中，可将现有基于量化的ANN方法加速最高达3.8倍。

Conclusion: BBC能有效增强现有基于量化的ANN索引在大k查询上的效率，通过改进候选维护机制和重排序过程，成功解决了大k ANN查询的性能瓶颈问题。

Abstract: Although Approximate Nearest Neighbor (ANN) search has been extensively studied, large-k ANN queries that aim to retrieve a large number of nearest neighbors remain underexplored, despite their numerous real-world applications. Existing ANN methods face significant performance degradation for such queries. In this work, we first investigate the reasons for the performance degradation of quantization-based ANN indexes: (1) the inefficiency of existing top-k collectors, which incurs significant overhead in candidate maintenance, and (2) the reduced pruning effectiveness of quantization methods, which leads to a costly re-ranking process. To address this, we propose a novel bucket-based result collector (BBC) to enhance the efficiency of existing quantization-based ANN indexes for large-k ANN queries. BBC introduces two key components: (1) a bucket-based result buffer that organizes candidates into buckets by their distances to the query. This design reduces ranking costs and improves cache efficiency, enabling high performance maintenance of a candidate superset and a lightweight final selection of top-k results. (2) two re-ranking algorithms tailored for different types of quantization methods, which accelerate their re-ranking process by reducing either the number of candidate objects to be re-ranked or cache misses. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that BBC accelerates existing quantization-based ANN methods by up to 3.8x at recall@k = 0.95 for large-k ANN queries.

</details>


### [62] [Optimizing Relational Queries over Array-Valued Data in Columnar Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01967)
*Maroua Zeblah,Etienne Couritas,Sarah Chlyah,Pierre Genevès,Nils Gesbert,Nabil Layaïda*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Modern analytical workloads increasingly combine relational data with array-valued attributes. While columnar database systems efficiently process such workloads, their ability to optimize queries that interleave relational operators with array manipulations remains limited. This paper introduces A3D-RA, an extended relational algebra supporting array-valued attributes, together with a comprehensive framework for algebraic reasoning and optimization. We formalize its data model and semantics, develop a complete set of equivalence-preserving transformation rules capturing pairwise interactions between relational and array operators, and propose a plan enumeration strategy with an optimality guarantee that remains polynomial in all non-join operators. We design A3D-RA as a modular, backend-independent optimization layer that can be instantiated over existing analytical database systems. Experimental results across three high-performance engines on a real-world workload show consistent performance gains enabled by the proposed algebraic optimization layer.

</details>

### [63] [ReFormeR: Learning and Applying Explicit Query Reformulation Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01417)
*Amin Bigdeli,Mert Incesu,Negar Arabzadeh,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ReFormeR是一种模式引导的查询重写方法，通过从查询对中提取可迁移的重写模式库，并基于检索上下文为新查询选择合适的模式来约束重写过程，而非直接让语言模型生成重写结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接让LLM生成查询改写，存在改写策略不明确、效果不稳定的问题。需要一种可控的、目标导向的查询重写方法。

Method: 1) 从初始查询与更强改写查询的配对中提取短重写模式；2) 将模式整合为紧凑的可迁移模式库；3) 根据新查询的检索上下文选择合适的模式；4) 用选定模式约束LLM进行目标性重写（如消歧、词汇落地、添加区分性方面等）。

Result: 在TREC DL 2019、DL 2020和DL Hard数据集上，ReFormeR相比传统反馈方法和近期LLM-based查询重写/扩展方法取得了一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过显式化改写策略，模式引导的查询重写方法能够引导LLM生成更有针对性和有效性的查询改写。

Abstract: We present ReFormeR, a pattern-guided approach for query reformulation. Instead of prompting a language model to generate reformulations of a query directly, ReFormeR first elicits short reformulation patterns from pairs of initial queries and empirically stronger reformulations, consolidates them into a compact library of transferable reformulation patterns, and then selects an appropriate reformulation pattern for a new query given its retrieval context. The selected pattern constrains query reformulation to controlled operations such as sense disambiguation, vocabulary grounding, or discriminative facet addition, to name a few. As such, our proposed approach makes the reformulation policy explicit through these reformulation patterns, guiding the LLM towards targeted and effective query reformulations. Our extensive experiments on TREC DL 2019, DL 2020, and DL Hard show consistent improvements over classical feedback methods and recent LLM-based query reformulation and expansion approaches.

</details>


### [64] [STABLE: Efficient Hybrid Nearest Neighbor Search via Magnitude-Uniformity and Cardinality-Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01617)
*Qianyun Yang,Zhiwei Chen,Yupeng Hu,Zixu Li,Zhiheng Fu,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: STABLE是一个用于大规模异构数据的混合近似最近邻搜索框架，通过AUTO度量解决相似度幅度异构性问题，通过HELP索引组织异构语义关系，并采用动态异构路由实现高效搜索，在多种属性基数的特征向量基准数据集上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前混合近似最近邻搜索方法忽视了数据分布的异构性，存在两个主要挑战：相似度幅度异构性导致的兼容性障碍，以及属性基数异构性带来的容忍瓶颈。这些问题导致现有方法在处理不同分布特征的数据集时准确性和鲁棒性不足。

Method: STABLE框架包含三个核心组件：1) AUTO度量（增强的异构语义感知度量），实现特征相似性与属性一致性的联合测量；2) HELP索引（异构图），基于AUTO组织异构语义关系；3) 动态异构路由方法，确保高效搜索。

Result: 在五个具有不同属性基数的特征向量基准数据集上进行了广泛实验，验证了STABLE在准确率、效率和鲁棒性方面的优越性能。

Conclusion: STABLE框架能够有效应对数据分布异构性挑战，为大规模异构数据场景下的混合近似最近邻搜索提供了一种准确、高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Hybrid Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search (Hybrid ANNS) is a foundational search technology for large-scale heterogeneous data and has gained significant attention in both academia and industry. However, current approaches overlook the heterogeneity in data distribution, thus ignoring two major challenges: the Compatibility Barrier for Similarity Magnitude Heterogeneity and the Tolerance Bottleneck to Attribute Cardinality. To overcome these issues, we propose the robuSt heTerogeneity-Aware hyBrid retrievaL framEwork, STABLE, designed for accurate, efficient, and robust hybrid ANNS under datasets with various distributions. Specifically, we introduce an enhAnced heterogeneoUs semanTic perceptiOn (AUTO) metric to achieve a joint measurement of feature similarity and attribute consistency, addressing similarity magnitude heterogeneity and improving robustness to datasets with various attribute cardinalities. Thereafter, we construct our Heterogeneous sEmantic reLation graPh (HELP) index based on AUTO to organize heterogeneous semantic relations. Finally, we employ a novel Dynamic Heterogeneity Routing method to ensure an efficient search. Extensive experiments on five feature vector benchmarks with various attribute cardinalities demonstrate the superior performance of STABLE.

</details>


### [65] [Multi-Agent Video Recommenders: Evolution, Patterns, and Open Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02211)
*Srivaths Ranganathan,Abhishek Dharmaratnakar,Anushree Sinha,Debanshu Das*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本论文是一篇关于多智能体视频推荐系统（MAVRS）的综述，涵盖从传统单模型推荐到基于LLM的多智能体架构的演进历程，提出分类体系，分析协作机制与代表框架，并指出可扩展性、多模态理解、激励对齐等开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单模型推荐系统优化静态参与度指标，难以满足现代平台动态需求；多智能体架构通过协调专业化智能体（视频理解、推理、记忆、反馈），提供更精准、可解释的推荐，成为重新定义视频推荐系统的关键范式。

Method: 采用综述方法，融合多智能体推荐系统、基础模型和对话AI的思想；提出协作模式分类体系；分析跨短视频、教育等不同视频领域的协调机制；对早期MARL系统（如MMRF）与近期LLM驱动架构（如MACRec、Agent4Rec）进行对比分析。

Result: 系统梳理了MAVRS领域发展脉络，建立了包含协调模式的技术分类体系；识别了视频理解、推理、记忆、反馈等核心智能体功能模块；总结了从MARL到LLM驱动的技术演进路径。

Conclusion: MAVRS领域面临可扩展性、多模态理解、激励对齐等开放挑战；未来研究方向包括混合RL-LLM系统、终身个性化推荐和自改进推荐系统。

Abstract: Video recommender systems are among the most popular and impactful applications of AI, shaping content consumption and influencing culture for billions of users. Traditional single-model recommenders, which optimize static engagement metrics, are increasingly limited in addressing the dynamic requirements of modern platforms. In response, multi-agent architectures are redefining how video recommender systems serve, learn, and adapt to both users and datasets. These agent-based systems coordinate specialized agents responsible for video understanding, reasoning, memory, and feedback, to provide precise, explainable recommendations. In this survey, we trace the evolution of multi-agent video recommendation systems (MAVRS). We combine ideas from multi-agent recommender systems, foundation models, and conversational AI, culminating in the emerging field of large language model (LLM)-powered MAVRS. We present a taxonomy of collaborative patterns and analyze coordination mechanisms across diverse video domains, ranging from short-form clips to educational platforms. We discuss representative frameworks, including early multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) systems such as MMRF and recent LLM-driven architectures like MACRec and Agent4Rec, to illustrate these patterns. We also outline open challenges in scalability, multimodal understanding, incentive alignment, and identify research directions such as hybrid reinforcement learning-LLM systems, lifelong personalization and self-improving recommender systems.

</details>


### [66] [From BM25 to Corrective RAG: Benchmarking Retrieval Strategies for Text-and-Table Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01733)
*Meftun Akarsu,Recep Kaan Karaman,Christopher Mierbach*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对10种检索策略在金融QA基准（23,088条查询，7,318份混合文本和表格的文档）上进行了系统性评测。两阶段混合检索+神经重排pipeline表现最佳（Recall@5=0.816, MRR@3=0.605）；BM25在金融文档中优于先进稠密检索；查询扩展和自适应检索对精确数值查询收益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统严重依赖检索质量，但针对异构文档（包含文本和表格）的现代检索方法缺乏系统性对比研究。现有基准测试未能充分覆盖金融领域常见的多模态内容场景。

Method: 在包含23,088条查询和7,318份混合文本-表格文档的金融QA基准上，对10种检索策略（稀疏检索、稠密检索、混合融合、跨编码器重排、查询扩展、索引增强、自适应检索）进行系统评测。采用Recall@k、MRR、nDCG评估检索质量，Number Match评估端到端生成质量，并使用配对Bootstrap显著性检验。

Result: 1. 两阶段pipeline（混合检索+神经重排）达Recall@5=0.816、MRR@3=0.605，显著优于所有单阶段方法；2. BM25在金融文档上优于先进稠密检索，挑战了语义搜索普遍优于传统检索的假设；3. 查询扩展方法（HyDE、多查询）和自适应检索对精确数值查询收益有限，而上下文检索则带来一致提升。

Conclusion: 对于金融领域RAG系统，建议采用混合检索+神经重排的两阶段架构；BM25在结构化金融文档中仍具竞争力；精确数值查询场景下应避免过度依赖查询扩展。研究成果包括消融实验、成本-精度权衡建议及开源基准代码。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems critically depend on retrieval quality, yet no systematic comparison of modern retrieval methods exists for heterogeneous documents containing both text and tabular data. We benchmark ten retrieval strategies spanning sparse, dense, hybrid fusion, cross-encoder reranking, query expansion, index augmentation, and adaptive retrieval on a challenging financial QA benchmark of 23,088 queries over 7,318 documents with mixed text-and-table content. We evaluate retrieval quality via Recall@k, MRR, and nDCG, and end-to-end generation quality via Number Match, with paired bootstrap significance testing. Our results show that (1) a two-stage pipeline combining hybrid retrieval with neural reranking achieves Recall@5 of 0.816 and MRR@3 of 0.605, outperforming all single-stage methods by a large margin; (2) BM25 outperforms state-of-the-art dense retrieval on financial documents, challenging the common assumption that semantic search universally dominates; and (3) query expansion methods (HyDE, multi-query) and adaptive retrieval provide limited benefit for precise numerical queries, while contextual retrieval yields consistent gains. We provide ablation studies on fusion methods and reranker depth, actionable cost-accuracy recommendations, and release our full benchmark code.

</details>


### [67] [Do We Need Bigger Models for Science? Task-Aware Retrieval with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01965)
*Florian Kelber,Matthias Jobst,Yuni Susanti,Michael Färber*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了小型语言模型结合精心设计的检索管道是否能替代超大规模模型用于科学研究应用。研究设计了轻量级检索增强框架，包含任务感知路由机制，整合全文论文和结构化元数据，使用紧凑指令微调模型生成带引用的回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学知识发现工具依赖具有数百亿参数的专有系统，限制了研究的可重复性和可及性。本文试图回答一个核心问题：在科学应用中，是否真的需要更大的模型？

Method: 设计轻量级检索增强框架：(1) 任务感知路由机制，根据输入查询选择专门的检索策略；(2) 整合全文科学论文和结构化学术元数据；(3) 使用紧凑指令微调语言模型生成带引用的回答。在多个学术任务上评估，包括单/多文档问答、生物医学问答（域转移场景）及科学文本压缩。

Result: 检索设计与模型规模是互补而非可互换的。检索设计可部分补偿较小模型的能力不足，但模型容量对复杂推理任务仍然至关重要。

Conclusion: 检索设计和任务感知架构是构建实用、可重复的学术助手的关键因素，可降低对超大规模专有模型的依赖。

Abstract: Scientific knowledge discovery increasingly relies on large language models, yet many existing scholarly assistants depend on proprietary systems with tens or hundreds of billions of parameters. Such reliance limits reproducibility and accessibility for the research community. In this work, we ask a simple question: do we need bigger models for scientific applications? Specifically, we investigate to what extent carefully designed retrieval pipelines can compensate for reduced model scale in scientific applications. We design a lightweight retrieval-augmented framework that performs task-aware routing to select specialized retrieval strategies based on the input query. The system further integrates evidence from full-text scientific papers and structured scholarly metadata, and employs compact instruction-tuned language models to generate responses with citations. We evaluate the framework across several scholarly tasks, focusing on scholarly question answering (QA), including single- and multi-document scenarios, as well as biomedical QA under domain shift and scientific text compression. Our findings demonstrate that retrieval and model scale are complementary rather than interchangeable. While retrieval design can partially compensate for smaller models, model capacity remains important for complex reasoning tasks. This work highlights retrieval and task-aware design as key factors for building practical and reproducible scholarly assistants.

</details>
