Result: Apriel-Reasoner在AIME 2025、GPQA、MMLU-Pro和LiveCodeBench上显著超越Apriel-Base；推理时可泛化至32K token；在保持性能的前提下产生30-50%更短的推理轨迹；匹配同规模强大开放权重模型的性能，同时降低token消耗。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一套完全可复现的多领域RLVR训练配方，通过自适应采样和难度感知长度惩罚有效解决了跨领域优化和效率问题。Apriel-Reasoner在准确率与token预算的帕累托前沿上实现突破，为高效推理模型的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Building general-purpose reasoning models using reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) across diverse domains has been widely adopted by frontier open-weight models. However, their training recipes and domain mixtures are often not disclosed. Joint optimization across domains poses significant challenges: domains vary widely in rollout length, problem difficulty and sample efficiency. Further, models with long chain-of-thought traces increase inference cost and latency, making efficiency critical for practical deployment. We present Apriel-Reasoner, trained with a fully reproducible multi-domain RL post-training recipe on Apriel-Base, a 15B-parameter open-weight LLM, across five domains using public datasets: mathematics, code generation, instruction following, logical puzzles and function calling. We introduce an adaptive domain sampling mechanism that preserves target domain ratios despite heterogeneous rollout dynamics, and a difficulty-aware extension of the standard length penalty that, with no additional training overhead, encourages longer reasoning for difficult problems and shorter traces for easy ones. Trained with a strict 16K-token output budget, Apriel-Reasoner generalizes to 32K tokens at inference and improves over Apriel-Base on AIME 2025, GPQA, MMLU-Pro, and LiveCodeBench while producing 30-50% shorter reasoning traces. It matches strong open-weight models of similar size at lower token cost, thereby pushing the Pareto frontier of accuracy versus token budget.

</details>


### [107] [Feature Weighting Improves Pool-Based Sequential Active Learning for Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02019)
*Dongrui Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于特征加权的主动学习回归(ALR)方法，通过利用岭回归系数对特征进行加权来改进样本选择的质量。该方法可应用于单任务和多任务回归场景，显著提升了现有ALR方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ALR方法在计算样本间距离时未考虑不同特征的重要性差异，导致样本选择次优。在标注预算有限的情况下，如何更有效地选择最具信息量的样本是一个关键挑战。

Method: 利用少量已标注样本训练岭回归模型，将回归系数作为特征权重融入样本间距离计算。对于单任务场景提出3种方法，多任务场景提出2种方法。特征加权策略可扩展至流式主动学习和分类问题。

Result: 实验表明，该方法几乎总是能改进4种现有ALR方法的性能，在单任务和多任务回归问题上均表现出色。

Conclusion: 基于回归系数的特征加权策略是一种简单有效的ALR增强方法，易于实现且适用范围广，可推广至其他主动学习场景。

Abstract: Pool-based sequential active learning for regression (ALR) optimally selects a small number of samples sequentially from a large pool of unlabeled samples to label, so that a more accurate regression model can be constructed under a given labeling budget. Representativeness and diversity, which involve computing the distances among different samples, are important considerations in ALR. However, previous ALR approaches do not incorporate the importance of different features in inter-sample distance computation, resulting in sub-optimal sample selection. This paper proposes three feature weighted single-task ALR approaches and two feature weighted multi-task ALR approaches, where the ridge regression coefficients trained from a small amount of previously labeled samples are used to weight the corresponding features in inter-sample distance computation. Experiments showed that this easy-to-implement enhancement almost always improves the performance of four existing ALR approaches, in both single-task and multi-task regression problems. The feature weighting strategy may also be easily extended to stream-based ALR, and classification algorithms.

</details>


### [108] [AA-SVD : Anchored and Adaptive SVD for Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02119)
*Atul Kumar Sinha,François Fleuret*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于低秩分解的大语言模型压缩框架，通过同时考虑原始输入分布偏移和端到端块级优化，解决现有SVD方法在激进压缩比下性能崩溃的问题，实现对十亿参数模型的快速无损压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于分解的压缩方法存在两个问题：仅在原始输入上优化会忽略上游压缩带来的分布偏移；仅在偏移输入上优化可能偏离原始输出。此外，现有方法逐层独立压缩，无法补偿累积误差。

Method: 方法核心在于三个方面：(1) 锚定原始输出同时显式建模输入分布偏移；(2) 在单层分解基础上进一步进行端到端的transformer块级优化；(3) 最小化块级输出失真，使压缩层能够联合补偿累积误差。

Result: 在大语言模型上的实验表明，该方法在各种压缩比下均一致优于现有SVD基线方法，且在激进压缩比下优势更加明显，而竞争方法则严重降级或完全失效。

Conclusion: 该方法为高效大规模模型部署提供了实用解决方案，可在无需重训练的情况下实现快速压缩 billion 参数级别模型。

Abstract: We introduce a fast low-rank factorization-based framework for compressing large language models that enables rapid compression of billion-parameter models without retraining. Unlike existing factorization-based approaches that optimize only on the original inputs, ignoring distribution shifts from upstream compression and thus propagating errors forward, or those that rely only on shifted inputs and risk drifting away from the original outputs, our approach accounts for both. Beyond individual layer compression, we further refine each transformer block end-to-end, minimizing block-level output distortion and allowing compressed layers to jointly compensate for accumulated errors. By anchoring each compressed layer to the original outputs while explicitly modeling input distribution shifts, our method finds a low-rank approximation that maintains functional equivalence with the original model. Experiments on large language models show that our method consistently outperforms existing SVD-based baselines across compression ratios, with the advantage becoming increasingly pronounced at aggressive compression budgets, where competing methods degrade substantially or collapse entirely, offering a practical solution for efficient, large-scale model deployment.

</details>


### [109] [Auction-Based Online Policy Adaptation for Evolving Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02151)
*Guruprerana Shabadi,Kaushik Mallik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于拍卖机制的多目标强化学习框架，用于解决目标动态变化场景下的自适应策略问题。通过为每个目标训练自私的本地策略，并利用拍卖机制让策略竞标行动执行权，实现动态可解释的目标权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习中，目标可能在运行时动态出现或消失，需要设计能够高效调整行为的自适应策略。现有方法要么需要完全重新训练，要么无法提供可解释的目标权衡机制。

Method: 提出模块化拍卖框架：1) 每个目标对应一个自私的本地策略；2) 策略根据当前状态紧迫性出价竞标行动执行权；3) 通过转化为一般和博弈来计算本地策略，使用PPO并发训练。目标变化时，通过增删对应策略实现即时适应。

Result: 在Atari Assault和动态目标网格世界路径规划任务中，本方法显著优于使用PPO训练的单体策略。

Conclusion: 基于拍卖的模块化框架能够有效解决目标动态变化的自适应问题，通过博弈论训练和竞标机制实现可解释的目标权衡。

Abstract: We consider multi-objective reinforcement learning problems where objectives come from an identical family -- such as the class of reachability objectives -- and may appear or disappear at runtime. Our goal is to design adaptive policies that can efficiently adjust their behaviors as the set of active objectives changes. To solve this problem, we propose a modular framework where each objective is supported by a selfish local policy, and coordination is achieved through a novel auction-based mechanism: policies bid for the right to execute their actions, with bids reflecting the urgency of the current state. The highest bidder selects the action, enabling a dynamic and interpretable trade-off among objectives. Going back to the original adaptation problem, when objectives change, the system adapts by simply adding or removing the corresponding policies. Moreover, as objectives arise from the same family, identical copies of a parameterized policy can be deployed, facilitating immediate adaptation at runtime. We show how the selfish local policies can be computed by turning the problem into a general-sum game, where the policies compete against each other to fulfill their own objectives. To succeed, each policy must not only optimize its own objective, but also reason about the presence of other goals and learn to produce calibrated bids that reflect relative priority. In our implementation, the policies are trained concurrently using proximal policy optimization (PPO). We evaluate on Atari Assault and a gridworld-based path-planning task with dynamic targets. Our method achieves substantially better performance than monolithic policies trained with PPO.

</details>


### [110] [On the Role of Depth in the Expressivity of RNNs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02201)
*Maude Lizaire,Michael Rizvi-Martel,Éric Dupuis,Guillaume Rabusseau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The benefits of depth in feedforward neural networks are well known: composing multiple layers of linear transformations with nonlinear activations enables complex computations. While similar effects are expected in recurrent neural networks (RNNs), it remains unclear how depth interacts with recurrence to shape expressive power. Here, we formally show that depth increases RNNs' memory capacity efficiently with respect to the number of parameters, thus enhancing expressivity both by enabling more complex input transformations and improving the retention of past information. We broaden our analysis to 2RNNs, a generalization of RNNs with multiplicative interactions between inputs and hidden states. Unlike RNNs, which remain linear without nonlinear activations, 2RNNs perform polynomial transformations whose maximal degree grows with depth. We further show that multiplicative interactions cannot, in general, be replaced by layerwise nonlinearities. Finally, we validate these insights empirically on synthetic and real-world tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Smoothing the Landscape: Causal Structure Learning via Diffusion Denoising Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02250)
*Hao Zhu,Di Zhou,Donna Slonim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DDCD框架将去噪得分匹配目标与自适应k-hop环路约束相结合，利用扩散模型的梯度平滑特性解决高维因果发现中的可扩展性和稳定性问题，无需矩阵求逆即可保证DAG结构约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果发现方法（如NOTEARS和DAG-GNN）在处理高维数据时存在可扩展性和稳定性问题，尤其在特征-样本不平衡情况下表现不佳。扩散模型的去噪得分匹配目标能够平滑梯度，有望改善收敛速度与稳定性。

Method: 1) 利用扩散模型的去噪得分匹配目标平滑梯度，加速收敛；2) 提出自适应k-hop环路约束替代矩阵求逆，降低计算复杂度；3) DDCD利用逆向去噪过程推断因果结构，而非生成数据。

Result: DDCD在合成基准数据上展现出竞争力，在两个真实世界案例的定性分析中证明了实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了高维因果发现的可扩展性与稳定性问题，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Understanding causal dependencies in observational data is critical for informing decision-making. These relationships are often modeled as Bayesian Networks (BNs) and Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs). Existing methods, such as NOTEARS and DAG-GNN, often face issues with scalability and stability in high-dimensional data, especially when there is a feature-sample imbalance. Here, we show that the denoising score matching objective of diffusion models could smooth the gradients for faster, more stable convergence. We also propose an adaptive k-hop acyclicity constraint that improves runtime over existing solutions that require matrix inversion. We name this framework Denoising Diffusion Causal Discovery (DDCD). Unlike generative diffusion models, DDCD utilizes the reverse denoising process to infer a parameterized causal structure rather than to generate data. We demonstrate the competitive performance of DDCDs on synthetic benchmarking data. We also show that our methods are practically useful by conducting qualitative analyses on two real-world examples. Code is available at this url: https://github.com/haozhu233/ddcd.

</details>


### [112] [Model-Based Reinforcement Learning for Control under Time-Varying Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02260)
*Klemens Iten,Bruce Lee,Chenhao Li,Lenart Treven,Andreas Krause,Bhavya Sukhija*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究非平稳动态系统下的强化学习控制问题，采用高斯过程动态模型，通过自适应数据缓冲机制限制过时数据的影响，在持续学习设置下实现动态遗憾保证，并在连续控制基准测试中验证了性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统因漂移、磨损或工况变化而违反平稳性假设，传统学习方法依赖平稳动态条件，无法适应随时间变化的系统特性，需要专门处理非平稳性的RL方法。

Method: 采用持续模型化RL框架和高斯过程动态模型，在频繁变化预算假设下分析问题；设计乐观模型化RL算法配合自适应数据缓冲机制，主动限制过时数据的权重以保持校准的不确定性。

Result: 理论分析表明，持续性非平稳性要求显式限制过时数据的影响以维持校准的不确定性和有意义的动态遗憾保证；实验验证了算法在非平稳动态连续控制基准测试中的性能改进。

Conclusion: 所提出的乐观模型化RL算法通过自适应数据缓冲有效应对非平稳动态，为时变系统控制提供了一种实用的学习控制方案。

Abstract: Learning-based control methods typically assume stationary system dynamics, an assumption often violated in real-world systems due to drift, wear, or changing operating conditions. We study reinforcement learning for control under time-varying dynamics. We consider a continual model-based reinforcement learning setting in which an agent repeatedly learns and controls a dynamical system whose transition dynamics evolve across episodes. We analyze the problem using Gaussian process dynamics models under frequentist variation-budget assumptions. Our analysis shows that persistent non-stationarity requires explicitly limiting the influence of outdated data to maintain calibrated uncertainty and meaningful dynamic regret guarantees. Motivated by these insights, we propose a practical optimistic model-based reinforcement learning algorithm with adaptive data buffer mechanisms and demonstrate improved performance on continuous control benchmarks with non-stationary dynamics.

</details>


### [113] [SKILL0: In-Context Agentic Reinforcement Learning for Skill Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02268)
*Zhengxi Lu,Zhiyuan Yao,Jinyang Wu,Chengcheng Han,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Weiming Lu,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SKILL0是一个用于将外部技能内部化到LLM Agent参数中的框架，通过上下文强化学习训练，使Agent能够在零样本情况下自主执行任务，无需运行时技能检索。该框架包含渐进式撤回技能上下文的课程学习和基于在策有用性评估的动态课程，在保持高效上下文（每步<0.5k tokens）的同时，显著提升Agent性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理时技能增强方法存在三大固有局限：检索噪声导致无关引导、注入技能内容带来大量token开销、以及模型仅是"遵循"技能而非真正"习得"知识。研究者探索将技能内部化到模型参数中，实现零样本自主行为。

Method: SKILL0采用两阶段课程学习框架：(1) 训练时课程从完整技能上下文开始，逐步撤回；(2) 动态课程评估每个技能文件的在策有用性，仅保留当前策略仍可从中受益的技能，在线性衰减预算内迭代，直到Agent达到完全零样本运行状态。技能按类别离线分组并与交互历史一起渲染为紧凑视觉上下文。

Result: 在ALFWorld和Search-QA任务上的Agent实验表明，SKILL0相比标准RL基线分别提升+9.7%和+6.6%，同时保持极高的上下文效率（每步少于0.5k tokens）。

Conclusion: 通过上下文强化学习实现技能内部化是可行且有效的，Agent可在不依赖运行时技能检索的情况下实现高性能表现，为解决LLM Agent技能增强的固有局限提供了新思路。

Abstract: Agent skills, structured packages of procedural knowledge and executable resources that agents dynamically load at inference time, have become a reliable mechanism for augmenting LLM agents. Yet inference-time skill augmentation is fundamentally limited: retrieval noise introduces irrelevant guidance, injected skill content imposes substantial token overhead, and the model never truly acquires the knowledge it merely follows. We ask whether skills can instead be internalized into model parameters, enabling zero-shot autonomous behavior without any runtime skill retrieval. We introduce SKILL0, an in-context reinforcement learning framework designed for skill internalization. SKILL0 introduces a training-time curriculum that begins with full skill context and progressively withdraws it. Skills are grouped offline by category and rendered with interaction history into a compact visual context, teaching he model tool invocation and multi-turn task completion. A Dynamic Curriculum then evaluates each skill file's on-policy helpfulness, retaining only those from which the current policy still benefits within a linearly decaying budget, until the agent operates in a fully zero-shot setting. Extensive agentic experiments demonstrate that SKILL0 achieves substantial improvements over the standard RL baseline (+9.7\% for ALFWorld and +6.6\% for Search-QA), while maintaining a highly efficient context of fewer than 0.5k tokens per step. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-REAL/SkillZero.

</details>


### [114] [Unifying Group-Relative and Self-Distillation Policy Optimization via Sample Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02288)
*Gengsheng Li,Tianyu Yang,Junfeng Fang,Mingyang Song,Mao Zheng,Haiyun Guo,Dan Zhang,Jinqiao Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SRPO方法，通过样本路由机制结合GRPO和SDPO的优势，并引入熵感知动态加权机制解决SDPO的晚期不稳定性问题，在5个基准测试上实现性能提升和计算效率优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法存在两大问题：GRPO的粗粒度信用分配对所有失败样本统一惩罚，缺乏token级精细指导；而SDPO虽能提供密集的logit级监督实现快速早期改进，但长期训练时容易出现性能崩溃。

Method: 提出Sample-Routed Policy Optimization (SRPO)框架：(1) 样本路由机制将正确样本导向GRPO的奖励对齐强化学习，将失败样本导向SDPO的定向logit级纠正；(2) 熵感知动态加权机制抑制高熵不可靠的蒸馏目标，强调高置信度目标。

Result: 在五个基准测试和两种模型规模上，SRPO实现了SDPO的快速早期改进和GRPO的长期稳定性。Qwen3-8B上平均性能提升3.4%（相比GRPO）和6.3%（相比SDPO），同时缩短响应长度并降低17.2%的每步计算成本。

Conclusion: SRPO通过统一框架有效融合两种方法的互补优势，解决了SDPO的晚期不稳定性问题，验证了样本级路由和熵感知动态加权策略的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a standard paradigm for post-training large language models. While Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely adopted, its coarse credit assignment uniformly penalizes failed rollouts, lacking the token-level focus needed to efficiently address specific deviations. Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) addresses this by providing denser, more targeted logit-level supervision that facilitates rapid early improvement, yet it frequently collapses during prolonged training. We trace this late-stage instability to two intrinsic flaws: self-distillation on already-correct samples introduces optimization ambiguity, and the self-teacher's signal reliability progressively degrades. To resolve these issues, we propose Sample-Routed Policy Optimization (SRPO), a unified on-policy framework that routes correct samples to GRPO's reward-aligned reinforcement and failed samples to SDPO's targeted logit-level correction. SRPO further incorporates an entropy-aware dynamic weighting mechanism to suppress high-entropy, unreliable distillation targets while emphasizing confident ones. Evaluated across five benchmarks and two model scales, SRPO achieves both the rapid early improvement of SDPO and the long-horizon stability of GRPO. It consistently surpasses the peak performance of both baselines, raising the five-benchmark average on Qwen3-8B by 3.4% over GRPO and 6.3% over SDPO, while simultaneously yielding moderate response lengths and lowering per-step compute cost by up to 17.2%.

</details>


### [115] [Taming the Exponential: A Fast Softmax Surrogate for Integer-Native Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02292)
*Dimitrios Danopoulos,Enrico Lupi,Michael Kagan,Maurizio Pierini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax (HCCS)，一种用于替代Transformer中Softmax函数的有界单调近似方法，专为AMD Versal AI Engines的int8硬件设计，可显著提升小模型低精度推理的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer的Multi-Head Attention (MHA)模块中，Softmax函数的指数运算和归一化在小模型低精度推理场景下开销巨大，成为计算瓶颈。AMD AI Engine平台现有参考实现依赖bfloat16或LUT来执行指数运算，无法充分利用平台的高吞吐量整数向量处理单元。

Method: 1. 设计HCCS：使用截断线性映射替代指数函数，基于最大中心注意力logits生成有界、单调的概率分布；2. 离线校准：为每个注意力头引入轻量级校准参数，基于代表性数据集优化以保持各头统计特性；3. 硬件映射：将HCCS自然映射到AI Engine的int8 MAC单元。

Result: 该方法首次在AMD AI引擎上实现int8优化的softmax替代方案，在小模型或重度量化MHA工作负载上经量化感知重训练后，任务精度具有竞争力，且吞吐量显著超越现有参考实现。

Conclusion: HCCS通过硬件感知的近似设计，在保持模型精度的同时有效解决了Transformer推理中Softmax的计算瓶颈问题，为低精度高吞吐量场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Softmax can become a computational bottleneck in the Transformer model's Multi-Head Attention (MHA) block, particularly in small models under low-precision inference, where exponentiation and normalization incur significant overhead. As such, we suggest using Head-Calibrated Clipped-Linear Softmax (HCCS), a bounded, monotone surrogate to the exponential softmax function, which uses a clipped linear mapping of the max centered attention logits. This approximation produces a stable probability distribution, maintains the ordering of the original logits and has non-negative values. HCCS differs from previous softmax surrogates as it includes a set of lightweight calibration parameters that are optimized offline based on a representative dataset and calibrated for each individual attention head to preserve the statistical properties of the individual heads. We describe a hardware-motivated implementation of HCCS for high-throughput scenarios targeting the AMD Versal AI Engines. The current reference implementations from AMD for this platform rely upon either bfloat16 arithmetic or LUTs to perform the exponential operation, which might limit the throughput of the platform and fail to utilize the high-throughput integer vector processing units of the AI Engine. In contrast, HCCS provides a natural mapping to the AI Engines' int8 multiply accumulate (MAC) units. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first int8 optimized softmax surrogate for AMD AI engines that significantly exceeds the speed performance of other reference implementations while maintaining competitive task accuracy on small or heavily quantized MHA workloads after quantization-aware retraining.

</details>


### [116] [go-$m$HC: Direct Parameterization of Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections via Generalized Orthostochastic Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02309)
*Torque Dandachi,Sophia Diggs-Galligan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出go-mHC方法，基于广义正交随机矩阵理论，以O(d³)复杂度精确参数化Birkhoff多面体，解决了精确方法与高效方法之间的表达力权衡问题。在合成任务和30M参数GPT模型上验证了方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有精确参数化Birkhoff多面体的方法随残差流数量d呈阶乘级增长，计算代价极高；而Kronecker分解方法虽高效但表达力受限。如何在O(d³)复杂度内实现完全表达力的精确参数化是关键挑战。

Method: 提出基于广义正交随机矩阵（generalized orthostochastic matrices）的精确参数化方法，引入超参数s实现从计算高效边界到完全表达Birkhoff多面体的连续插值。在mHC框架上实现为go-mHC，可与Kronecker分解方法自然组合。

Result: go-mHC在Birkhoff多面体内占据更完整的空间；合成流混合任务中达到理论最小损失，收敛速度提升10倍；30M参数GPT风格语言模型验证有效。

Conclusion: go-mHC在表达力、效率和精确性三方面取得平衡，为将d作为模型容量的新维度提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Doubly stochastic matrices enable learned mixing across residual streams, but parameterizing the set of doubly stochastic matrices (the Birkhoff polytope) exactly and efficiently remains an open challenge. Existing exact methods scale factorially with the number of streams ($d$), while Kronecker-factorized approaches are efficient but expressivity-limited. We introduce a novel exact parameterization grounded in the theory of generalized orthostochastic matrices, which scales as $\mathcal{O}(d^3)$ and exposes a single hyperparameter $s$ which continuously interpolates between a computationally efficient boundary and the fully expressive Birkhoff polytope. Building on Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections ($m$HC), a framework for learned dynamic layer connectivity, we instantiate this parameterization in go-$m$HC. Our method composes naturally with Kronecker-factorized methods, substantially recovering expressivity at similar FLOP costs. Spectral analysis indicates that go-$m$HC fills the Birkhoff polytope far more completely than Kronecker-factorized baselines. On synthetic stream-mixing tasks, go-$m$HC achieves the minimum theoretical loss while converging up to $10\times$ faster. We validate our approach in a 30M parameter GPT-style language model. The expressivity, efficiency, and exactness of go-$m$HC offer a practical avenue for scaling $d$ as a new dimension of model capacity.

</details>


### [117] [Batched Contextual Reinforcement: A Task-Scaling Law for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02322)
*Bangji Yang,Hongbo Ma,Jiajun Fan,Ge Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出批量上下文强化学习（BCR），通过训练LLM在共享上下文窗口中同时解决N个问题来隐式创建token预算，在降低15.8%-62.6% token消耗的同时保持或提升准确率，实现了"免费午餐"现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维推理虽性能优异但token消耗过大，导致推理成本高企。现有效率优化方法（长度惩罚、难度估计器、多阶段课程）存在降低推理质量或需要复杂训练流程的缺陷。

Method: 提出批量上下文强化学习（BCR），一种单阶段训练范式。核心修改：训练模型在共享上下文窗口中同时解决N个问题，仅以每实例准确率为奖励。隐式token预算机制使模型自主调节效率。

Result: （1）发现新任务-扩展律：N增大时每问题token消耗单调下降，准确率比基线更平稳衰减；（2）1.5B和4B模型系列均实现"免费午餐"：token减少15.8%-62.6%，五个数学基准测试准确率持平或提升；（3）定性分析显示模型自发消除冗余元认知循环；（4）隐式预算约束成功规避显式长度惩罚的对抗性梯度和灾难性优化崩溃。

Conclusion: BCR证明简单的结构激励可释放LLM潜在的高密度推理能力，提供稳定、无需复杂训练的约束型长度控制方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models employing Chain-of-Thought reasoning achieve strong performance but suffer from excessive token consumption that inflates inference costs. Existing efficiency methods such as explicit length penalties, difficulty estimators, or multi-stage curricula either degrade reasoning quality or require complex training pipelines. We introduce Batched Contextual Reinforcement, a minimalist, single-stage training paradigm that unlocks efficient reasoning through a simple structural modification: training the model to solve N problems simultaneously within a shared context window, rewarded purely by per-instance accuracy. This formulation creates an implicit token budget that yields several key findings: (1) We identify a novel task-scaling law: as the number of concurrent problems N increases during inference, per-problem token usage decreases monotonically while accuracy degrades far more gracefully than baselines, establishing N as a controllable throughput dimension. (2) BCR challenges the traditional accuracy-efficiency trade-off by demonstrating a "free lunch" phenomenon at standard single-problem inference. Across both 1.5B and 4B model families, BCR reduces token usage by 15.8% to 62.6% while consistently maintaining or improving accuracy across five major mathematical benchmarks. (3) Qualitative analyses reveal emergent self-regulated efficiency, where models autonomously eliminate redundant metacognitive loops without explicit length supervision. (4) Crucially, we empirically demonstrate that implicit budget constraints successfully circumvent the adversarial gradients and catastrophic optimization collapse inherent to explicit length penalties, offering a highly stable, constraint-based alternative for length control. These results prove BCR practical, showing simple structural incentives unlock latent high-density reasoning in LLMs.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Near-Optimal Parallel Approximate Counting via Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01263)
*David G. Harris,Vladimir Kolmogorov,Hongyang Liu,Yitong Yin,Yiyao Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 论文研究近似计数与采样的计算等价性问题，提出新的非自适应和两轮自适应算法，样本复杂度分别为 O(q log²h/ε²) 和 O(q logh/ε²)，优于已知方法，并给出 RNC 并行计数算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优的模拟退火计数算法本质上是顺序的或自适应的，查询的参数 β 依赖于先前样本。研究目标是开发非自适应或低自适应的算法，在保持相近样本复杂度的同时实现高效并行计算。

Method: 开发简单的非自适应近似计数算法，以及仅需两轮自适应性的算法。两种方法均基于 partition function 比例 Q = Z(β_max)/Z(β_min) 的估计。

Result: 非自适应算法使用 O(q log²h/ε²) 样本；两轮自适应算法使用 O(q logh/ε²) 样本，匹配顺序算法的最优样本复杂度。两种算法均可转化为工作高效的并行 RNC 计数算法。

Conclusion: 成功消除了自适应需求，实现高效并行计数算法，可应用于反铁磁 2 自旋、单体-二聚体和铁磁伊辛模型等多种经典模型的 RNC 计数问题。

Abstract: The computational equivalence between approximate counting and sampling is well established for polynomial-time algorithms. The most efficient general reduction from counting to sampling is achieved via simulated annealing, where the counting problem is formulated in terms of estimating the ratio $Q={Z(β_{\max})}/{Z(β_{\min})}$ between partition functions $Z(β)=\sum_{x\in Ω} \exp(βH(x))$ of Gibbs distributions $μ_β$ over $Ω$ with Hamiltonian $H$, given access to a sampling oracle that produces samples from $μ_β$ for $β\in [β_{\min}, β_{\max}]$.
  The best bound achieved by known annealing algorithms with relative error $\varepsilon$ is $O(q \log h / \varepsilon^2)$, where $q, h$ are parameters which respectively bound $\ln Q$ and $H$. However, all known algorithms attaining this near-optimal complexity are inherently sequential, or *adaptive*: the queried parameters $β$ depend on previous samples.
  We develop a simple non-adaptive algorithm for approximate counting using $O(q \log^2 h / \varepsilon^2)$ samples, as well as an algorithm that achieves $O(q \log h / \varepsilon^2)$ samples with just two rounds of adaptivity, matching the best sample complexity of sequential algorithms. These algorithms naturally give rise to work-efficient parallel (RNC) counting algorithms.
  We discuss applications to RNC counting algorithms for several classic models, including the anti-ferromagnetic 2-spin, monomer-dimer and ferromagnetic Ising models.

</details>


### [119] [Space-Efficient Text Indexing with Mismatches using Function Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01307)
*Jackson Bibbens,Levi Borevitz,Samuel McCauley*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究在O(n)空间约束下，如何高效回答字符串的Hamming距离查询问题。通过将Fiat-Naor函数求逆技术应用于CGL树，提出了一个O(n)空间的数据结构，查询时间约为O(|q| + log^{4k}n + log^{2k}n·#occ)，且与字母表大小无关。这是k≥3时线性空间下已知最优的查询时间，同时给出了空间-时间的平滑权衡以及首个亚线性空间结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGL树虽然具有优秀的查询时间复杂度，但其空间复杂度为O(n log^k n)。最近空间被优化到O(n log^{k-1}n)，但自然的问题是：在O(n)空间的约束下，查询效率能有多高？此前的研究虽然有进展，但查询时间对n、k和字母表Σ的依赖都不理想。本文旨在回答这个问题。

Method: 主要技术思路是将Fiat-Naor的函数求逆技术应用于CGL树。这一结合并非直接，需要重新审视Fiat-Naor数据结构和平滑权衡技术以及CGL树的描述，以获得更好的理论边界。

Result: 提出了一个O(n)空间的数据结构，查询时间约为O(|q| + log^{4k}n + log^{2k}n·#occ)，且与|Σ|无关。即使|Σ| = O(1)，当k≥3且#occ不大时，这也是已知最好的线性空间查询时间。同时给出了首个亚线性空间结果——仅使用o(n)额外空间的简洁数据结构。

Conclusion: 本文在O(n)空间约束下实现了最优的查询时间（k≥3时），提供了空间-时间的平滑权衡，并首次实现了亚线性空间的Hamming距离字符串索引。文中对Fiat-Naor数据结构和CGL树的改进也具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: A classic data structure problem is to preprocess a string T of length $n$ so that, given a query $q$, we can quickly find all substrings of T with Hamming distance at most $k$ from the query string. Variants of this problem have seen significant research both in theory and in practice. For a wide parameter range, the best worst-case bounds are achieved by the "CGL tree" (Cole, Gottlieb, Lewenstein 2004), which achieves query time roughly $\tilde{O}(|q| + \log^k n + \# occ)$ where $\# occ$ is the size of the output, and space ${O}(n\log^k n)$. The CGL Tree space was recently improved to $O(n \log^{k-1} n)$ (Kociumaka, Radoszewski 2026).
  A natural question is whether a high space bound is necessary. How efficient can we make queries when the data structure is constrained to $O(n)$ space? While this question has seen extensive research, all known results have query time with unfavorable dependence on $n$, $k$, and the alphabet $Σ$. The state of the art query time (Chan et al. 2011) is roughly $\tilde{O}(|q| + |Σ|^k \log^{k^2 + k} n + \# occ)$.
  We give an $O(n)$-space data structure with query time roughly $\tilde{O}(|q| + \log^{4k} n + \log^{2k} n \# occ)$, with no dependence on $|Σ|$. Even if $|Σ| = O(1)$, this is the best known query time for linear space if $k\geq 3$ unless $\# occ$ is large. Our results give a smooth tradeoff between time and space. We also give the first sublinear-space results: we give a succinct data structure using only $o(n)$ space in addition to the text itself.
  Our main technical idea is to apply function inversion (Fiat, Naor 2000) to the CGL tree. Combining these techniques is not immediate; in fact, we revisit the exposition of both the Fiat-Naor data structure and the CGL tree to obtain our bounds. Along the way, we obtain improved performance for both data structures, which may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [120] [A divide and conquer strategy for multinomial particle filter resampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01356)
*Andrey A. Popov*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对粒子滤波器重采样的新型多项式重采样方法，特别适用于所需样本数小于等于底层离散分布规模的情况（如高斯混合滤波器）。通过计算复杂度分析和数值实验，证明了该方法优于两种已知最佳多项式采样方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在集成混合模型滤波器（如高斯混合滤波器）中，经常需要从离散分布中提取少于等于分布规模的样本，但现有多项式重采样方法在此场景下并非最优。

Method: 提出一种新的多项式重采样程序，针对样本数小于等于底层离散分布规模的特定情况进行优化。

Result: 该方法在计算复杂度上优于两种已知最佳的多项式采样程序，并通过数值实验验证了其优越性。

Conclusion: 新方法在特定重采样场景下具有更好的性能表现，可应用于粒子滤波器和集成混合模型滤波器。

Abstract: This work provides a new multinomial resampling procedure for particle filter resampling, focused on the case where the number of samples required is less than or equal to the size of the underlying discrete distribution. This setting is common in ensemble mixture model filters such as the Gaussian mixture filter. We show superiority of our approach with respect two of the best known multinomial sampling procedures both through a computational complexity analysis and through a numerical experiment.

</details>


### [121] [Approximating the Permanent of a Random Matrix with Polynomially Small Mean: Zeros and Universality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01367)
*Frederic Koehler,Pui Kuen Leung*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究当矩阵元素略微偏离零时，如何高效近似随机矩阵的积式(permanent)。通过分析随机多项式 per(zJ+W) 的零点分布，建立了半径为 Õ(n^(-1/3)) 的无零点区域，从而在偏置为 Ω̃(n^(-1/3)) 时实现高效近似算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解线性光学和玻色采样(boson sampling)的经典复杂性。Barvinok的插值方法要求建立足够大的无零点区域，但此前在偏置小于 1/polylog(n) 时没有高效算法，且尚不清楚是否存在模大于等于1的零点。

Method: 1) 分析复高斯随机矩阵 W 对应的多项式 per(zJ+W) 的零点分布；2) 使用复分析工具控制零点位置；3) 对非高斯情形建立通用性结果（亚指数分布的i.i.d.条目）。

Result: 1) 零点全部位于半径为 Õ(n^(-1/3)) 的圆盘内；2) (1-ε)n 个零点的模为 Θ(n^(-1/2))；3) 当偏置为 Ω̃(n^(-1/3)) 时存在高效近似算法；4) 对于一般图上的硬核模型建立类似无零点区域；5) 对亚指数分布条目证明通用性。

Conclusion: 将可高效近似的最小偏置从 1/polylog(n) 降低到 Õ(n^(-1/3))，揭示了零点分布的精细结构（多数零点模为 Θ(n^(-1/2))），从而避免与积式近似平均情况复杂度的猜想矛盾。

Abstract: We study algorithms for approximating the permanent of a random matrix when the entries are slightly biased away from zero. This question is motivated by the goal of understanding the classical complexity of linear optics and \emph{boson sampling} (Aaronson and Arkhipov '11; Eldar and Mehraban '17). Barvinok's interpolation method enables efficient approximation of the permanent, provided one can establish a sufficiently large zero-free region for the polynomial $\mathrm{per}(zJ + W)$, where $J$ is the all-ones matrix and $W$ is a random matrix with independent mean-zero entries.
  We show that when the entries of $W$ are standard complex Gaussians, all zeros of the random polynomial $\mathrm{per}(zJ + W)$ lie within a disk of radius $\tilde{O}(n^{-1/3})$, which yields an approximation algorithm when the bias of the entries is $\tildeΩ(n^{-1/3})$. Previously, there were no efficient algorithms at biases smaller than $1/\mathrm{polylog}(n)$, and it was unknown whether there typically exist zeros $z$ with $|z| \ge 1$. As a complementary result, we show that the bulk of the zeros, namely $(1 - ε)n$ of them, have magnitude $Θ(n^{-1/2})$. This prevents our interpolation method from contradicting the conjectured average-case hardness of approximating the permanent. We also establish analogous zero-free regions for the hardcore model on general graphs with complex vertex fugacities. In addition, we prove universality results establishing zero-free regions for random matrices $W$ with i.i.d. subexponential entries.

</details>


### [122] [A Simple Average-case Analysis of Recursive Randomized Greedy MIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01462)
*Mina Dalirrooyfard,Konstantin Makarychev,Slobodan Mitrović*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文重新分析了递归版随机贪心最大独立集（MIS）算法，证明了每顶点期望递归调用次数受平均度上界约束，但采用了比原证明更简单的势函数方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yoshida等人（2009）的原始分析复杂精妙但难以理解，本文旨在提供更简洁透明的分析方法。

Method: 借鉴Dalirrooyfard、Makarychev和Mitrović（2024）在相关聚类算法中开发的势函数方法，将其适配到MIS场景。

Result: 获得与原分析相同的上界保证：每顶点期望递归调用次数不超过图的平均度。

Conclusion: 势函数方法为递归随机贪心MIS算法提供了更直接、更透明的分析框架。

Abstract: We revisit the complexity analysis of the recursive version of the randomized greedy algorithm for computing a maximal independent set (MIS), originally analyzed by Yoshida, Yamamoto, and Ito (2009). They showed that, on average per vertex, the expected number of recursive calls made by this algorithm is upper bounded by the average degree of the input graph. While their analysis is clever and intricate, we provide a significantly simpler alternative that achieves the same guarantee.
  Our analysis is inspired by the recent work of Dalirrooyfard, Makarychev, and Mitrović (2024), who developed a potential-function-based argument to analyze a new algorithm for correlation clustering. We adapt this approach to the MIS setting, yielding a more direct and arguably more transparent analysis of the recursive randomized greedy MIS algorithm.

</details>


### [123] [Sublinear-query relative-error testing of halfspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01557)
*Xi Chen,Anindya De,Yizhi Huang,Shivam Nadimpalli,Rocco A. Servedio,Tianqi Yang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了稀疏布尔值函数的相对误差性质测试模型，重点关注在实数域 $\mathbb{R}^n$ 上、高斯分布 $N(0, I_n)$ 下对半空间（线性阈值函数）进行测试。与已知在布尔域上需要 $\Omega(\log n)$ 次查询的下界形成对比，本文证明了在实数高斯域上可以用亚线性（sublinear）查询复杂度测试半空间，且远少于学习所需的查询数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对误差性质测试模型是 [CDHLNSY24] 为研究"稀疏"布尔值函数（即只有一小部分输入满足函数）的测试而引入的框架。[CDHNSY26] 已证明在布尔域 $\{0,1\}^n$ 上，任何半空间的相对误差测试算法都需要 $\Omega(\log n)$ 次 oracle 调用。本文旨在为该下界提供上界补充——在实数高斯分布下证明存在更高效的测试算法。

Method: 作者综合运用了多种数学工具：Hermite 分析（用于函数展开和正交多项式技术）、高斯等周不等式（用于体积和边界估计）以及噪声敏感性和表面积的几何结果（用于分析函数在噪声下的行为）。这些工具共同用于构建和证明关于半空间测试复杂度的算法。

Result: 在实数域 $\mathbb{R}^n$、标准高斯分布 $N(0, I_n)$ 下，半空间可以通过亚线性（sublinear）数量的 oracle 调用进行相对误差测试。这一复杂度显著低于 [CDHNSY26] 在布尔域上证明的 $\Omega(\log n)$ 下界，也远少于纯粹学习半空间所需的查询数量。

Conclusion: 本文填补了相对误差测试研究中关于半空间的上界空白，揭示了测试复杂度与分布/域的选择之间的深刻联系：同一函数类在布尔域和高斯域上具有截然不同的测试复杂度。这表明在设计性质测试算法时，分布假设和域结构是关键的考量因素。

Abstract: The relative-error property testing model was introduced in [CDHLNSY24] to facilitate the study of property testing for "sparse" Boolean-valued functions, i.e. ones for which only a small fraction of all input assignments satisfy the function. In this framework, the distance from the unknown target function $f$ that is being tested to a function $g$ is defined as $\mathrm{Vol}(f \mathop{\triangle} g)/\mathrm{Vol}(f)$, where the numerator is the fraction of inputs on which $f$ and $g$ disagree and the denominator is the fraction of inputs that satisfy $f$.
  Recent work [CDHNSY26] has shown that over the Boolean domain $\{0,1\}^n$, any relative-error testing algorithm for the fundamental class of halfspaces (i.e. linear threshold functions) must make $Ω(\log n)$ oracle calls. In this paper we complement the [CDHNSY26] lower bound by showing that halfspaces can be relative-error tested over $\mathbb{R}^n$ under the standard $N(0,I_n)$ Gaussian distribution using a sublinear number of oracle calls -- in particular, substantially fewer than would be required for learning. Our results use a wide range of tools including Hermite analysis, Gaussian isoperimetric inequalities, and geometric results on noise sensitivity and surface area.

</details>


### [124] [Single-Pass Streaming CSPs via Two-Tier Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01575)
*Amir Azarmehr,Soheil Behnezhad,Shane Ferrante*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究在流式计算模式下，用次线性空间实现Max-CSP的近似算法。通过two-tier sampling技术，证明可达到BasicLP integrality gap α的近似比，空间复杂度为n^(1-Ω(1))，从而完全解决了一个重要猜想的第一部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式设置下Max-CSP问题：约束顺序任意到达，需单趟处理逼近最大可满足约束比例。已知存在O(n)空间的平凡近优解，核心问题是o(n)空间下能实现何种近似比。

Method: 提出two-tier sampling技术：结合edge sampling和vertex sampling同时处理高低度顶点；通过在约简图上模拟合适的局部算法实现。

Result: 证明对于任意固定ε>0，可用n^(1-Ω_ε(1))空间单趟实现(α-ε)-近似。其中α为BasicLP的integrality gap。

Conclusion: 完全解决了一个重要猜想的第一部分：通过不同于ABFS26的全新算法恢复了Max-DiCut的结果，同时该技术可推广至一般Max-CSP。

Abstract: We study the maximum constraint satisfaction problem, Max-CSP, in the streaming setting. Given $n$ variables, the constraints arrive sequentially in an arbitrary order, with each constraint involving only a small subset of the variables. The objective is to approximate the maximum fraction of constraints that can be satisfied by an optimal assignment in a single pass. The problem admits a trivial near-optimal solution with $O(n)$ space, so the major open problem in the literature has been the best approximation achievable when limiting the space to $o(n)$.
  The answer to the question above depends heavily on the CSP instance at hand. The integrality gap $α$ of an LP relaxation, known as the BasicLP, plays a central role. In particular, a major conjecture of the area is that in the single-pass streaming setting, for any fixed $\varepsilon > 0$, (i) an $(α-\varepsilon)$-approximation can be achieved with $o(n)$ space, and (ii) any $(α+\varepsilon)$-approximation requires $Ω(n)$ space.
  In this work, we fully resolve the first side of the conjecture by proving that an $(α- \varepsilon)$-approximation of Max-CSP can indeed be achieved using $n^{1-Ω_\varepsilon(1)}$ space and in a single pass. Given that Max-DiCut is a special case of Max-CSP, our algorithm fully recovers the recent result of [ABFS26, STOC'26] via a completely different algorithm and proof. On a technical level, our algorithm simulates a suitable local algorithm on a reduced graph using a technique that we call *two-tier sampling*: the algorithm combines both edge sampling and vertex sampling to handle high- and low-degree vertices at the same time.

</details>


### [125] [Adaptive Fully Dynamic $k$-Center Clustering with (Near-)Optimal Worst-Case Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01726)
*Mara Grilnberger,Antonis Skarlatos*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了全动态k-center聚类问题在对抗性点插入和删除下的算法设计，证明可以在自适应对抗者模型下同时实现常数近似比、近最优最坏情况更新时间以及常数资源追溯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：能否在对抗性点插入和删除序列下，同时优化k-clustering问题的近似比、更新时间和追溯（recourse）？此前工作仅能在遗忘型对手下给出期望摊销保证，无法应对自适应对抗者。k-center聚类作为最经典的研究问题之一，亟需解决这一理论空白。

Method: 研究方法分为两步：(1) 开发一个全动态双准则近似算法，具有(近)最优的最坏情况界限，分别建立最坏情况追溯和最坏情况更新时间的保证；(2) 在此基础上设计适合的全动态k-center算法，通过简单而精妙的过程将两者合并。

Result: 证明全动态k-center聚类问题在自适应对抗者模型下可实现：常数因子近似、近最优最坏情况更新时间、常数最坏情况追溯。该结果完整回答了文中提出的两个核心问题，填补了此前研究的空白。

Conclusion: 该工作作为首个在自适应对抗者模型下实现全部三项指标最优化的全动态k-center聚类算法，为动态聚类问题的鲁棒算法设计确立了新的基准。

Abstract: Given a sequence of adversarial point insertions and point deletions, is it possible to simultaneously optimize the approximation ratio, update time, and recourse for a $k$-clustering problem? If so, can this be achieved with worst-case guarantees against an adaptive adversary? These questions have garnered significant attention in recent years.
  Prior works by Bhattacharya, Costa, Garg, Lattanzi, and Parotsidis [FOCS '24] and by Bhattacharya, Costa, and Farokhnejad [STOC '25] have taken significant steps toward this direction for the $k$-median clustering problem and its generalization, the $(k, z)$-clustering problem. In this paper, we study the $k$-center clustering problem, which is one of the most classical and well-studied $k$-clustering problems. Recently, Bhattacharya, Costa, Farokhnejad, Lattanzi, and Parotsidis [ICML '25] provided an affirmative answer to the first question for the $k$-center clustering problem. However, their work did not resolve the second question, as their result provides only expected amortized guarantees against an oblivious adversary.
  In this work, we make significant progress and close the gap by answering both questions in the affirmative. Specifically, we show that the fully dynamic $k$-center clustering problem admits a constant-factor approximation, near-optimal worst-case update time, and constant worst-case recourse, even against an adaptive adversary. This is achieved by first developing a fully dynamic bicriteria approximation algorithm with (near-)optimal worst-case bounds, and then designing a suitable fully dynamic $k$-center algorithm with near-linear update time. For the fully dynamic bicriteria approximation algorithm, we establish the worst-case recourse and worst-case update time guarantees separately, and then merge them into a single algorithm through a simple yet elegant process.

</details>


### [126] [A Constant-Approximation Distance Labeling Scheme under Polynomially Many Edge Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01829)
*Bernhard Haeupler,Yaowei Long,Antti Roeyskoe,Thatchaphol Saranurak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 提出首个能在任意数量边故障下达到常数近似比的容错距离标注方案，改进了先前方案仅能达到O(f)近似比的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有容错距离标注方案只能提供关于故障数f的线性近似比，无法达到常数近似。作者旨在解决Dory和Parter在DP21中提出的开放问题——是否存在能处理任意数量边故障的常数近似容错距离标注方案。

Method: 设计了一种确定性多项式时间算法，构造包含O(k^4)近似比和̃O(f^4 n^{1/k})标签大小的距离标注方案。

Result: 首次实现常数近似比（O(k^4)）同时能处理任意数量边故障的容错距离标注。该方案也改进了距离敏感 oracle 的现有最优结果。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了容错距离标注领域的核心开放问题，在距离敏感 oracle 领域（特别是f = Θ(log n)故障场景）也取得了显著进展，消除了先前方案中查询时间超线性、近似比线性依赖f或指数级k依赖等限制。

Abstract: A fault-tolerant distance labeling scheme assigns a label to each vertex and edge of an undirected weighted graph $G$ with $n$ vertices so that, for any edge set $F$ of size $|F| \leq f$, one can approximate the distance between $p$ and $q$ in $G \setminus F$ by reading only the labels of $F \cup \{p,q\}$.
  For any $k$, we present a deterministic polynomial-time scheme with $O(k^{4})$ approximation and $\tilde{O}(f^{4}n^{1/k})$ label size. This is the first scheme to achieve a constant approximation while handling any number of edge faults $f$, resolving the open problem posed by Dory and Parter [DP21]. All previous schemes provided only a linear-in-$f$ approximation [DP21, LPS25].
  Our labeling scheme directly improves the state of the art in the simpler setting of distance sensitivity oracles. Even for just $f = Θ(\log n)$ faults, all previous oracles either have super-linear query time, linear-in-$f$ approximation [CLPR12], or exponentially worse $2^{{\rm poly}(k)}$ approximation dependency in $k$ [HLS24].

</details>


### [127] [Probabilistic AVL Trees (p-AVL): Relaxing Deterministic Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02223)
*Hayagriv Desikan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了p-AVL树的经验行为，这是一种AVL树的概率变体，其中每个不平衡以概率p进行修复，实现从BST（p=0）到标准AVL树（p=1）的连续插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVL树是自平衡二叉搜索树，但其严格的平衡维护导致较高的旋转开销。研究希望探索是否可以通过概率性平衡修复来获得介于标准BST和AVL树之间的性能权衡，同时降低维护成本。

Method: 通过随机顺序插入实验，追踪以下指标：每个节点的旋转次数、总不平衡事件数、平均深度、平均高度以及全局不平衡统计量σ。

Result: 主要经验发现是：即使很小的非零p值也会导致强烈的结构变化。

Conclusion: 研究目标偏重经验性而非完全理论性，旨在清晰记录p-AVL树族的行为并识别主要模式。

Abstract: This paper studies the empirical behaviour of the p-AVL tree, a probabilistic variant of the AVL tree in which each imbalance is repaired with probability $p$. This gives an exact continuous interpolation from $p = 0$, which recovers the BST endpoint, to $p = 1$, which recovers the standard AVL tree. Across random-order insertion experiments, we track rotations per node, total imbalance events, average depth, average height, and a global imbalance statistic $σ$. The main empirical result is that even small nonzero p already causes a strong structural change. The goal here is empirical rather than fully theoretical: to document the behaviour of the p-AVL family clearly and identify the main patterns.

</details>


### [128] [Subquadratic Counting via Perfect Marginal Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02235)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Zongchen Chen,Kuikui Liu,Xinyuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究自旋系统配分函数的近似计算复杂度，提出新算法打破传统的O(n²)时间屏障。对于硬核模型（最大度Δ图的λ加权独立集），当 fugacity λ < 1/(Δ-1) 时，得到亚二次时间 O(n^{2-δ}) 的近似计数算法，并将结果推广到伊辛模型、超图独立集和顶点着色等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于标准计数-采样归约的技术需要 O(n²) 时间（n为输入图大小），效率较低。Anand等(2025)针对硬核模型在 λ = o(Δ^{-3/2}) 条件下取得亚二次算法，但适用范围有限。本文旨在进一步扩展亚二次算法的适用范围。

Method: 核心方法揭示亚二次计数与完美边缘采样（perfect marginal sampling）之间的深层联系：1) 对两自旋系统（如硬核、伊辛模型），完美边缘采样器可直接（黑盒方式）产生亚二次计数算法；2) 对一般自旋系统，将现有完美边缘采样器适配为亚线性时间内产生低方差边缘估计器。

Result: 1. 硬核模型：当 λ < 1/(Δ-1) 时，获得亚二次近似计数算法（常数 δ > 0），改进了前人的 λ = o(Δ^{-3/2}) 条件；2. 广泛适用于伊辛模型、超图独立集、顶点着色等多种自旋系统。

Conclusion: 亚二次计数与完美边缘采样之间存在深层理论联系：完美边缘采样器的存在可直接（或经适当适配）转化为亚二次时间计数算法，这一发现为设计高效近似计数算法提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We study the computational complexity of approximately computing the partition function of a spin system. Techniques based on standard counting-to-sampling reductions yield $\tilde{O}(n^2)$-time algorithms, where $n$ is the size of the input graph. We present new counting algorithms that break the quadratic-time barrier in a wide range of settings. For example, for the hardcore model of $λ$-weighted independent sets in graphs of maximum degree $Δ$, we obtain a $\tilde{O}(n^{2-δ})$-time approximate counting algorithm, for some constant $δ> 0$, when the fugacity $λ< \frac{1}{Δ-1}$, improving over the previous regime of $λ= o(Δ^{-3/2})$ by Anand, Feng, Freifeld, Guo, and Wang (2025). Our results apply broadly to many other spin systems, such as the Ising model, hypergraph independent sets, and vertex colorings.
  Interestingly, our work reveals a deep connection between $\textit{subquadratic}$ counting and $\textit{perfect}$ marginal sampling. For two-spin systems such as the hardcore and Ising models, we show that the existence of perfect marginal samplers directly yields subquadratic counting algorithms in a $\textit{black-box}$ fashion. For general spin systems, we show that almost all existing perfect marginal samplers can be adapted to produce a sufficiently low-variance marginal estimator in sublinear time, leading to subquadratic counting algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Training-Free Private Synthesis with Validation: A New Frontier for Practical Educational Data Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01821)
*Hibiki Ito,Chia-Yu Hsu,Hiroaki Ogata*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实用的两阶段差分隐私合成数据生成(DP-SDG)方法，用于教育领域的真实世界数据共享。该方法结合了基于LLM的无训练合成数据生成和按需真实数据验证，旨在降低隐私保护数据共享的技术门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育领域真实世界数据(RWD)的二次利用面临隐私限制和数据碎片化的挑战。现有的深度学习DP-SDG方法需要大量工程工作，且对小样本高维数据效果有限，难以实现实用的下游效用。

Method: 提出两阶段方法：(1)基于LLM的无训练差分隐私合成数据生成，实现合成数据共享；(2)按需真实数据验证，研究人员提交代码进行远程结果验证。方法支持单次和多轮合成场景。实验采用三年收集的真实数据评估，并与深度学习方法对比。

Result: LLM-based DP-SDG与深度学习方法性能相当，同时大幅降低工程成本；非DP验证造成可测量但适度的隐私泄露。案例研究中，研究人员报告平均仅36%的合成数据发现能在真实数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 该方法为教育RWD共享提供了实用方案，但仍存在风险缓解和认识论精确性方面的挑战。未来需改进隐私风险控制，提高验证覆盖率。

Abstract: While secondary use of real-world data (RWD) in education offers substantial research opportunities, data sharing is often limited by privacy constraints. Differentially private synthetic data generation (DP-SDG) has emerged as a possible solution. However, educational RWD is fragmented across platforms and institutions and stored in different formats, so DP-SDG must be tailored to each dataset, requiring substantial engineering effort. In addition, such data are often small-sample and high-dimensional, making deep learning (DL)-based methods common but difficult to implement without specialist expertise. In this setting, it is also hard to achieve practically useful downstream utility. As a result, despite its theoretical promise, DP-SDG remains far from a practical solution in education. To address this issue, we propose a more practical two-stage method: (1) training-free, LLM-based DP-SDG is performed for sharing synthetic data and (2) on-demand real-data validation, where researchers submit code for remote validation of results. This simple method is designed for individual data custodians without extensive DP-SDG expertise. It can also be adapted to multi-shot synthesis, where data from different learner cohorts are synthesised regularly. We evaluate this method experimentally in both the one-shot and multi-shot synthesis settings using RWD collected over three years and conduct a case study with real researchers. Results show that LLM-based DP-SDG performs comparably to a DL-based baseline while greatly reducing engineering costs, and that non-DP validation causes measurable but moderate privacy leakage. Nonetheless, in the case study researchers reported that on average only 36% of synthetic findings are validated on real data. Overall, the paper provides a practical method for sharing educational RWD, while highlighting challenges in risk mitigation and epistemic precision.

</details>


### [130] [Teaching Students to Question the Machine: An AI Literacy Intervention Improves Students' Regulation of LLM Use in a Science Task](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01955)
*O. Clerc,R. Abdelghani,C. Desvaux,E. Poisson,P. Y. Oudeyer,H. Sauzéon*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究探讨了两小时AI素养工作坊对初中生在使用大语言模型（LLM）辅助科学问题解决中的影响，发现短期可扩展的干预训练能显著改善学生的AI交互策略和表现，但对元认知自评分数无显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在教育中的快速普及引发担忧：初中生的元认知调节能力尚在发展中，容易过度信任AI输出。由于课堂时间和教师培训资源有限，亟需开发可在现实学校条件下实施的AI素养干预方案。

Method: 采用对照组实验设计。116名8-9年级学生（13-15岁）使用生成式AI系统完成六项科学探究任务。干预组在任务前两天参加两小时工作坊（内容涵盖LLM工作原理、局限性、提示策略及响应评估）；对照组无培训。通过编码分析交互行为、准确性判断和最终表现来评估效果。

Result: 接受培训的学生表现出更少的盲目依赖行为：更频繁地重新表述查询、提出跟进问题、更准确地判断响应正确性，最终表现更好。相比之下，GenAI使用量和元认知自评分数均无法预测表现。

Conclusion: 简短、可扩展的AI素养教学能有效改善初中生在类课堂学习活动中使用生成式AI的方式。有效使用LLM更多取决于交互调节的显性训练，而非自我报告类指标。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) in schools raises concerns about students' uncritical reliance on its outputs. Effective use of large language models (LLMs) requires not only technical knowledge but also the ability to monitor, evaluate, and regulate one's interaction with the system, processes closely tied to metacognitive regulation. These skills are still developing in middle school, making students particularly vulnerable to over-trust and premature acceptance of AI outputs. Because classroom time and teacher training resources are constrained, there is a pressing need to develop and evaluate AI literacy interventions that can be implemented under realistic school conditions. We report a controlled classroom study examining whether a two-hour AI literacy workshop improves students' interaction strategies and quality of final answers in LLM-supported science problem solving. A total of 116 students (grades 8-9; ages 13-15) completed six science investigation tasks using a generative AI system. Two days prior, the intervention group attended the workshop, which combined information about how LLMs work and fail with practical guidance on prompting and response evaluation; the control group received no training. Trained students showed less uncritical reliance on the system: they more often reformulated queries, asked follow-up questions, and more accurately judged response correctness, leading to better performance. In contrast, GenAI and metacognitive self-report scores did not predict performance, suggesting that effective use of generative AI depends less on self-reported measures and more on explicit training in interaction regulation. Overall, the results show that brief, scalable AI literacy instruction can meaningfully improve how middle-school students use generative AI in school-like learning activities.

</details>


### [131] [Generative AI Spotlights the Human Core of Data Science: Implications for Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.02238)
*Nathan Taback*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文论述了生成式AI时代数据科学教育的核心方向：GAI可自动化执行数据清洗、可视化、建模等常规工作流，但问题界定、因果识别、伦理判断等高阶能力仍不可替代。作者基于Donoho的"更大数据科学"框架分析指出，计算与数据（GDS3）已大幅自动化，而数据收集准备（GDS1）和关于数据科学的科学（GDS6）仍需人类主导。教育启示是课程应聚焦人类核心能力，并训练学生在RAG迭代循环中有效协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI（GAI）正在自动化许多常规数据科学工作流，但数据科学教育应如何应对这一变革？作者认为技术进步应强化而非弱化对人类推理的关注，需要重新明确数据科学教育中"不可替代的人类核心"是什么。

Method: （1）追溯数据科学三条汇交脉络：图基的数据分析科学愿景、监控资本主义的商业逻辑、学术项目发展；（2）将GAI影响映射到Donoho GDS六分框架（数据探索GDS1、计算与数据GDS3、数据科学科学GDS6）；（3）借鉴Nolan和Temple Lang的计算素养观及McLuhan-Culkin工具塑造论。

Result: GDS3（计算与数据）已被大幅自动化；GDS1（数据获取、准备、探索）和GDS6（关于数据科学的科学）仍需关键人类输入。人类不可替代的核心能力包括：问题界定、测量与设计、因果识别、统计与计算推理、伦理与问责、意义建构。

Conclusion: 数据科学课程应聚焦人类核心能力，教授学生如何在检索增强生成（RAG）的迭代提示-输出-提示循环中有效贡献，学习成果与评估应明确考查推理与判断能力。

Abstract: Generative AI (GAI) reveals an irreducible human core at the center of data science: advances in GAI should sharpen, rather than diminish, the focus on human reasoning in data science education. GAI can now execute many routine data science workflows, including cleaning, summarizing, visualizing, modeling, and drafting reports. Yet the competencies that matter most remain irreducibly human: problem formulation, measurement and design, causal identification, statistical and computational reasoning, ethics and accountability, and sensemaking. Drawing on Donoho's Greater Data Science framework, Nolan and Temple Lang's vision of computational literacy, and the McLuhan-Culkin insight that we shape our tools and thereafter our tools shape us, this paper traces the emergence of data science through three converging lineages: Tukey's intellectual vision of data analysis as a science, the commercial logic of surveillance capitalism that created industrial demand for data scientists, and the academic programs that followed. Mapping GAI's impact onto Donoho's six divisions of Greater Data Science shows that computing with data (GDS3) has been substantially automated, while data gathering, preparation, and exploration (GDS1) and science about data science (GDS6) still require essential human input. The educational implication is that data science curricula should focus on this human core while teaching students how to contribute effectively within iterative prompt-output-prompt cycles using retrieval-augmented generation, and that learning outcomes and assessments should explicitly evaluate reasoning and judgment.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [132] [Identifying Privacy Concerns in Upcoming Software Release: A Peek into the Future](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01393)
*Aurek Chattopadhyay,Nan Niu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Pre-PI是一种在软件版本发布前识别隐私问题的新方法，通过将现有功能映射到语义相似的隐私评论来学习功能-隐私评论关系，从而模拟待发布功能的反馈并生成隐私问题摘要，优于依赖发布后用户反馈的现有方法Hark。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法只能在软件新功能发布后从应用商店获取用户反馈后才能分析隐私问题，无法在发布前帮助开发者和发布经理识别隐私问题，导致错失主动缓解隐私风险的时机。

Method: Pre-PI首先将现有功能映射到语义相似的隐私评论以学习功能-隐私评论关系，然后模拟待发布候选功能的反馈，最后生成简洁的隐私问题摘要。

Result: Pre-PI在三个真实应用上的评估显示，其能够生成额外的有效隐私问题，且识别时间早于Hark方法。

Conclusion: Pre-PI能够在上线前主动识别和缓解隐私问题，弥补了现有方法只能进行事后分析的不足。

Abstract: Identifying the features to be released in the next version of software, from a pool of potential candidates, is a challenging problem. User feedback from app stores is frequently used by software vendors for the evolution of apps across releases. Privacy feedback, although smaller in volume, carries a larger impact influencing app's success. Multiple existing work has focused on summarizing privacy concerns at the app level and has also shown that developers utilize feedback to implement security and privacy-related changes in subsequent releases. However, the current literature offers little support for release managers and developers in identifying privacy concerns prior to release. This gap exists as user reviews are typically available in app stores only after new features of a software system is released. In this paper, we introduce Pre-PI, a novel approach that summarizes privacy concerns for to-be-released features. Our method first maps existing features to semantically similar privacy reviews to learn feature-privacy review relations. We then simulate feedback for candidate features and generate concise summaries of privacy concerns. We evaluate Pre-PI across three real-world apps, and compare it with Hark, a state-of-the-art method that relies on post-release user feedback to identify privacy concerns. Results show that Pre-PI generates additional valid privacy concerns and identifies these concerns earlier than Hark, allowing proactive mitigation prior to release.

</details>


### [133] [AI Engineering Blueprint for On-Premises Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01395)
*Nicolas Weeger,Jakob Winkler,Annika Stiehl,Jóakim von Kistowski,Christian Uhl,Stefan Geißelsöder*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向企业本地部署的可扩展RAG（检索增强生成）系统完整AI工程蓝图，采用4+1视图模型描述端到端参考架构，并提供开源参考应用与CI/CD最佳实践，旨在帮助企业在严格数据保护法规下构建私有化RAG解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前企业RAG系统面临两大挑战：一是数据保护法规限制导致许多组织无法使用云服务，必须采用本地部署；二是现有架构蓝图和参考设计主要面向云端部署，缺乏企业级组件支持，导致全面的本地部署实施框架严重匮乏。

Method: 该研究提出一套完整的AI工程蓝图，包含三个核心组成部分：(1) 采用4+1视图模型描述端到端参考架构；(2) 提供可本地部署的参考应用程序；(3) 编制工具链、开发和CI/CD流水线的最佳实践。相关资源已在GitHub上公开发布。

Result: 产出了面向本地部署场景的可扩展企业RAG解决方案完整蓝图，涵盖架构设计、应用实现和工程实践三大维度，并通过行业合作伙伴的案例研究和专家访谈持续验证其实用价值。

Conclusion: 该蓝图填补了企业本地RAG部署框架的空白，通过标准化架构和开源实现降低了企业级RAG系统的部署门槛，未来将通过实践案例进一步评估其在真实企业环境中的效益。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems are gaining traction in enterprise settings, yet stringent data protection regulations prevent many organizations from using cloud-based services, necessitating on-premises deployments. While existing blueprints and reference architectures focus on cloud deployments and lack enterprise-grade components, comprehensive on-premises implementation frameworks remain scarce.
  This paper aims to address this gap by presenting a comprehensive AI engineering blueprint for scalable on-premises enterprise RAG solutions. It is designed to address common challenges and streamline the integration of RAG into existing enterprise infrastructure. The blueprint provides: (1) an end-to-end reference architecture described using the 4+1 view model, (2) a reference application for on-premises deployment, and (3) best practices for tooling, development, and CI/CD pipelines, all publicly available on GitHub. Ongoing case studies and expert interviews with industry partners will assess its practical benefits.

</details>


### [134] [Reproducible, Explainable, and Effective Evaluations of Agentic AI for Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01437)
*Jingyue Li,André Storhaug*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于软件工程中Agentic AI评估的立场论文（position paper），通过分析18篇顶会论文，识别了当前评估方法的局限，并提出了包括公开TAR轨迹和LLM交互数据在内的可复现、可解释评估指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI研究中，底层LLM如同黑盒运作，难以证明其相对于基线方法的优势；同时评估设计描述中的信息缺失使得结果复现不可行。

Method: 分析了18篇发表或接收于ICSE 2026、ICSE 2025、FSE 2025、ASE 2025和ISSTA 2025的论文，识别当前评估实践的现状与局限。

Result: 识别了当前及未来研究中评估Agentic AI for SE的主流方法及其局限性。

Conclusion: 提出了一套指南和建议，推荐研究者公开Thought-Action-Result (TAR)轨迹和LLM交互数据（或其摘要版本），以支持后续研究有效分析不同Agentic AI方法的优劣。提供了一个概念验证案例研究展示TAR轨迹如何支持跨方法的系统性分析。

Abstract: With the advancement of Agentic AI, researchers are increasingly leveraging autonomous agents to address challenges in software engineering (SE). However, the large language models (LLMs) that underpin these agents often function as black boxes, making it difficult to justify the superiority of Agentic AI approaches over baselines. Furthermore, missing information in the evaluation design description frequently renders the reproduction of results infeasible. To synthesize current evaluation practices for Agentic AI in SE, this study analyzes 18 papers on the topic, published or accepted by ICSE 2026, ICSE 2025, FSE 2025, ASE 2025, and ISSTA 2025. The analysis identifies prevailing approaches and their limitations in evaluating Agentic AI for SE, both in current research and potential future studies. To address these shortcomings, this position paper proposes a set of guidelines and recommendations designed to empower reproducible, explainable, and effective evaluations of Agentic AI in software engineering. In particular, we recommend that Agentic AI researchers make their Thought-Action-Result (TAR) trajectories and LLM interaction data, or summarized versions of these artifacts, publicly accessible. Doing so will enable subsequent studies to more effectively analyze the strengths and weaknesses of different Agentic AI approaches. To demonstrate the feasibility of such comparisons, we present a proof-of-concept case study that illustrates how TAR trajectories can support systematic analysis across approaches.

</details>


### [135] [Fuzzing with Agents? Generators Are All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01442)
*Vasudev Vikram,Rohan Padhye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Gentoo系统，利用LLM编码智能体自动合成针对特定模糊测试目标的输入生成器，探索AI是否能替代人工编写复杂的领域特定生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于生成器的模糊测试技术面临挑战：人工编写高度定制化的领域特定生成器需要大量时间和精力来编码程序的结构和语义逻辑。本研究旨在探索AI编码智能体是否能自动合成此类目标特定生成器，以及合成后的生成器是否足以替代传统的覆盖率引导变异策略。

Method: Gentoo系统由LLM编码智能体组成，可访问终端和目标模糊测试目标及其库的源代码，被指示迭代合成和优化输入生成器，并可选择性地提供细粒度的谓词级覆盖率反馈。实验评估了三种Gentoo配置，针对7个真实世界Java库的模糊测试目标，并与人工编写的生成器进行对比。

Result: 在7个基准测试中，智能体合成的生成器在4个上实现了统计上显著更高的分支覆盖率。关键发现：覆盖率引导和变异策略对智能体合成的生成器没有统计显著益处，但对所有人写生成器都有显著作用，表明智能体生成器编码的结构和语义逻辑使得覆盖率引导变得不必要。

Conclusion: AI编码智能体能够自动合成高质量的领域特定输入生成器，其编码的语义逻辑足以覆盖深层程序路径，从而无需依赖覆盖率引导和变异策略即可达到良好的测试效果。

Abstract: Modern generator-based fuzzing techniques combine lightweight input generators with coverage-guided mutation as a method of exploring deep execution paths in a target program. A complimentary approach in prior research focuses on creating highly customized, domain-specific generators that encode structural and semantic logic sufficient enough to reach deep program states; the challenge comes from the overhead of writing and testing these complex generators. We investigate whether AI coding agents can automatically synthesize such target-specific generators, and whether the resulting generators are strong enough to obviate the need for coverage guidance and mutation entirely.
  Our approach, Gentoo, is comprised of an LLM coding agent (provided terminal access and source code of the fuzz target and its library) instructed to iteratively synthesize and refine an input generator, and optionally provided fine-grained predicate-level coverage feedback. We evaluate three configurations of Gentoo against human-written generators on fuzz targets for 7 real-world Java libraries. Our findings show that agent-synthesized generators achieve statistically significantly higher branch coverage than human-written baseline generators on 4 of 7 benchmarks. Critically, the use of coverage guidance and mutation strategies is not statistically significantly beneficial for agent-synthesized generators, but is significant for all human-written generators, suggesting that structural and semantic logic encoded in the agent generators makes coverage guidance largely unnecessary.

</details>


### [136] [MOVis: A Visual Analytics Tool for Surfacing Missed Patches Across Software Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01494)
*Jorge Gonzalo Delgado Cervantes,John Businge,Daniel Ogenrwot*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MOVis是一款交互式桌面工具，用于可视化软件变体间的错失机会(MO)补丁。该工具加载PaReco的MO分类结果，以并排对比方式展示修复和缺陷代码块，突出显示对应区域，帮助开发者快速定位遗漏修复、理解所需适配，从而更高效地在软件变体间维护一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clone-and-own开发模式产生的软件变体家族各自独立演化，导致一个仓库中的重要修复常缺失于其他变体。PaReco研究表明此类MO补丁在真实生态系统中数以千计，但其文本输出对理解和定位修复传播的位置和方式支持有限。

Method: MOVis加载PaReco的MO分类结果，以并排视图呈现修补和缺陷代码块，高亮显示对应区域，暴露阻碍重用的结构性差异。采用轻量级交互式桌面工具设计。

Result: 开发者可快速定位遗漏修复、理解所需适配、高效维护软件变体间一致性。工具、复现包及演示视频已公开可用。

Conclusion: MOVis通过可视化方法有效弥补了PaReco文本输出的不足，为软件变体维护提供了更直观的分析和决策支持。

Abstract: Clone-and-own development produces families of related software variants that evolve independently. As variants diverge, important fixes applied in one repository are often missing in others. PaReco has shown that thousands of such missed opportunity (MO) patches exist across real ecosystems, yet its textual output provides limited support for understanding where and how these fixes should be propagated. We present MOVis, a lightweight, interactive desktop tool that visualizes MO patches between a source and target variant. MOVis loads PaReco's MO classifications and presents patched and buggy hunks side-by-side, highlighting corresponding regions and exposing structural differences that hinder reuse. This design enables developers to quickly locate missed fixes, understand required adaptations, and more efficiently maintain consistency across software variants. The tool, replication package, and demonstration video are available at https://zenodo.org/records/18356553 and https://youtu.be/Ac-gjBxHJ3Y.

</details>


### [137] [From SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO: Execution-free to Execution-based Fine-tuning for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01496)
*Nikolai Ludwig,Wasi Uddin Ahmad,Somshubra Majumdar,Boris Ginsburg*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO是一个两阶段SFT方案，通过蒸馏开源前沿LLM（Qwen3-Coder-480B）实现SWE-bench最优性能。SWE-ZERO侧重执行无关的大规模轨迹学习代码语义，SWE-HERO则进行执行信号引导的精细调优。该方案发布的SWE-HERO-32B模型在SWE-bench Verified达到62.2%准确率，并在多语言任务上展现44.1%的零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件工程智能体面临资源消耗大、泛化能力不足的问题。当前开源模型在SWE-bench上的表现与闭源前沿模型存在显著差距，需要探索更高效的训练范式。

Method: 两阶段SFT管道：(1) SWE-ZERO阶段：利用大规模执行无关轨迹数据（约300k条），习得代码语义理解和仓库级推理能力；(2) SWE-HERO阶段：使用精细的执行反馈信号（约13k条）进行针对性强化，实现语义直觉向严格工程工作流的转化。数据蒸馏源为Qwen3-Coder-480B，模型基座采用Qwen2.5-Coder系列。

Result: SWE-HERO-32B在SWE-bench Verified上达到62.2%解决率，刷新同规模开源模型记录。尽管仅在Python数据上训练，模型在SWE-bench Multilingual多语言基准上仍实现44.1%的零样本迁移准确率。

Conclusion: 通过执行无关与执行引导两阶段分离的蒸馏策略，可有效将大规模语言模型的代码能力迁移至软件工程任务。该范式具有良好的语言无关泛化特性，为构建高效软件工程智能体提供了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-ZERO to SWE-HERO, a two-stage SFT recipe that achieves state-of-the-art results on SWE-bench by distilling open-weight frontier LLMs. Our pipeline replaces resource-heavy dependencies with an evolutionary refinement strategy: (1) SWE-ZERO utilizes large-scale, execution-free trajectories to master code semantics and repository-level reasoning, and (2) SWE-HERO applies targeted, execution-backed refinement to transition these semantic intuitions into rigorous engineering workflows. Our empirical results set a new benchmark for open-source models of comparable size. We release a dataset of 300k SWE-ZERO and 13k SWE-HERO trajectories distilled from Qwen3-Coder-480B, alongside a suite of agents based on the Qwen2.5-Coder series. Notably, SWE-HERO-32B achieves a 62.2% resolution rate on SWE-bench Verified. Furthermore, despite being trained exclusively on Python, our agents demonstrate robust zero-shot transferability on SWE-bench Multilingual, reaching 44.1% and confirming the paradigm's generalizability across diverse languages.

</details>


### [138] [ToolMisuseBench: An Offline Deterministic Benchmark for Tool Misuse and Recovery in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01508)
*Akshey Sigdel,Rista Baral*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Tool using agents often fail for operational reasons even when language understanding is strong. Common causes include invalid arguments, interface drift, weak recovery, and inefficient retry behavior. We introduce ToolMisuseBench, an offline deterministic benchmark for evaluating tool misuse and recovery under explicit step, call, and retry budgets. The benchmark covers CRUD, retrieval, file, and scheduling environments with replayable fault injection. It reports success, invalid call behavior, policy violations, recovery quality, and budgeted efficiency. We release a public dataset with 6800 tasks and a reproducible evaluation pipeline. Baseline results show fault specific recovery gains for schema aware methods, while overall success remains limited under the released authorization and hard failure settings.

</details>


### [139] [Are Benchmark Tests Strong Enough? Mutation-Guided Diagnosis and Augmentation of Regression Suites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01518)
*Chenglin Li,Yisen Xu,Zehao Wang,Shin Hwei Tan,Tse-Hsun,Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Benchmarks driven by test suites, notably SWE-bench, have become the de facto standard for measuring the effectiveness of automated issue-resolution agents: a generated patch is accepted whenever it passes the accompanying regression tests. In practice, however, insufficiently strong test suites can admit plausible yet semantically incorrect patches, inflating reported success rates. We introduce STING, a framework for targeted test augmentation that uses semantically altered program variants as diagnostic stressors to uncover and repair weaknesses in benchmark regression suites. Variants of the ground-truth patch that still pass the existing tests reveal under-constrained behaviors; these gaps then guide the generation of focused regression tests. A generated test is retained only if it (i) passes on the ground-truth patch, (ii) fails on at least one variant that survived the original suite, and (iii) remains valid under behavior-preserving transformations designed to guard against overfitting. Applied to SWE-bench Verified, STING finds that 77% of instances contain at least one surviving variant. STING produces 1,014 validated tests spanning 211 instances and increases patch-region line and branch coverage by 10.8% and 9.5%, respectively. Re-assessing the top-10 repair agents with the strengthened suites lowers their resolved rates by 4.2%-9.0%, revealing that a substantial share of previously passing patches exploit weaknesses in the benchmark tests rather than faithfully implementing the intended fix. These results underscore that reliable benchmark evaluation depends not only on patch generation, but equally on test adequacy.

</details>


### [140] [EpiDroid: Dependency-Guided Recomposition for Deep State Discovery in Mobile GUI Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01522)
*Jiahui Song,Jiaxin Zhi,Kangjia Zhao,Chen Zhi,Junxiao Han,Xinkui Zhao,Nan Wang,Shuiguang Deng,Jianwei Yin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EpiDroid通过语义状态依赖感知增强现有移动应用GUI探索器，通过将原始轨迹提炼为稳定测试片段并使用LLM进行影响推理的重组-回放范式，迭代优化状态依赖图以访问深层应用状态。在20个真实应用上的实验表明，该框架一致性地提升所有基线工具性能，代码覆盖率提高10-28%，覆盖率增益是继续运行基线的3-4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化GUI探索方法忽视了测试片段之间的状态依赖关系，导致探索冗余且无法访问深层应用状态。

Method: EpiDroid采用黑盒可插拔架构：(1)将原始轨迹提炼为稳定测试片段以提取底层依赖；(2)使用重组-回放范式，通过LLM进行影响推理并在高价值可变状态元素上进行确定性回放；(3)通过迭代反馈机制逐步优化状态依赖图。

Result: 集成到工业和研究工具后，在20个真实应用中测试，结果显示EpiDroid一致性地提升所有基线性能，平均代码覆盖率提高10-28%，覆盖率增益是基线工具继续运行3-4倍。

Conclusion: 依赖导向的重组策略能够解锁前向探索无法访问的深层状态，该方法不依赖于额外的测试预算，具有通用性。

Abstract: The increasing scale and complexity of mobile applications make automated GUI exploration essential for software quality assurance. However, existing methods often neglect state dependencies between test fragments, which leads to redundant exploration and prevents access to deep application states. We introduce EpiDroid, a black-box, pluggable framework that augments existing explorers through semantic state dependency awareness. EpiDroid distills raw traces into stable test fragments to extract underlying dependencies. It then employs a Recomposition-Replay paradigm to perform impact reasoning via LLM and deterministic replay on high-value mutable state elements. Through iterative feedback, EpiDroid refines the state-dependency graph to systematically reach deep application states. We integrated EpiDroid into both industrial and state-of-the-art research tools and evaluated it on 20 real-world apps. The results show that EpiDroid consistently improves the performance of all baselines, increasing average code coverage by 10--28\% and delivering 3--4$\times$ more coverage gain compared to continuing the baselines alone from the same starting point. This demonstrates that dependency-guided recomposition unlocks deep states that forward exploration cannot access, irrespective of additional budget.

</details>


### [141] [ProdCodeBench: A Production-Derived Benchmark for Evaluating AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01527)
*Smriti Jha,Matteo Paltenghi,Chandra Maddila,Vijayaraghavan Murali,Shubham Ugare,Satish Chandra*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ProdCodeBench——一个从生产环境AI编程助手的真实会话中构建的基准测试，用于评估AI编码智能体。相较于现有基准测试，该基准更贴近实际生产工作负载，在编程语言分布、提示风格和代码库结构上与真实使用场景一致。通过对四个基础模型的系统性评测（解决率53.2%-72.2%），发现更频繁使用工作验证工具（如测试执行和静态分析）的模型表现更优，提示迭代验证对实现有效智能体行为的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试与真实生产环境在使用场景上存在显著差异，主要体现在编程语言分布、提示风格和代码库结构三个方面。这导致在工业环境中使用现有基准评估AI编码智能体时，无法准确反映其在实际生产场景中的表现能力。因此需要一种更贴近真实使用场景的评估方法。

Method: 1. 从生产AI编程助手的真实会话中收集数据；2. 使用LLM进行任务分类；3. 通过测试相关性验证筛选有效样本；4. 进行多轮运行稳定性检查以确保评估信号可靠性；5. 最终构建包含原始提示、提交代码变更和失败-通过测试的样本，涵盖七种编程语言。

Result: 四个基础模型的解决率在53.2%到72.2%之间。表现更好的模型展现出更频繁使用工作验证工具（如测试执行、静态分析调用）的特征，表明迭代验证机制对提升智能体性能有重要作用。

Conclusion: 迭代验证有助于实现有效的智能体行为。在陌生环境中，让外部训练的智能体接触更多代码库特定的验证机制，可能显著提升其性能。研究成果包括可复现的方法论和经验教训，供其他组织构建类似的生产源基准测试。

Abstract: Benchmarks that reflect production workloads are better for evaluating AI coding agents in industrial settings, yet existing benchmarks differ from real usage in programming language distribution, prompt style and codebase structure. This paper presents a methodology for curating production-derived benchmarks, illustrated through ProdCodeBench - a benchmark built from real sessions with a production AI coding assistant. We detail our data collection and curation practices including LLM-based task classification, test relevance validation, and multi-run stability checks which address challenges in constructing reliable evaluation signals from monorepo environments. Each curated sample consists of a verbatim prompt, a committed code change and fail-to-pass tests spanning seven programming languages. Our systematic analysis of four foundation models yields solve rates from 53.2% to 72.2% revealing that models making greater use of work validation tools, such as executing tests and invoking static analysis, achieve higher solve rates. This suggests that iterative verification helps achieve effective agent behavior and that exposing codebase-specific verification mechanisms may significantly improve the performance of externally trained agents operating in unfamiliar environments. We share our methodology and lessons learned to enable other organizations to construct similar production-derived benchmarks.

</details>


### [142] [Mitigating Implicit Inconsistencies in Patch Porting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01680)
*Shengyi Pan,Zhongxin Liu,Jiayuan Zhou,Xing Hu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MIP是一个基于LLM的自动化补丁迁移框架，通过结合编译器诊断和代码片段匹配来解决补丁从源代码库迁移到变体代码库时的隐式不一致性问题，实验表明其效果优于现有最佳基线两倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有补丁迁移方法主要处理补丁局部上下文中的可见不一致性，对于需要代码库间全局映射知识的不一致问题（称为隐式不一致）缺乏有效解决方案，而这类问题对开发者而言更难解决。

Method: MIP采用混合策略：(1) 当目标代码库中存在源标识符时，利用编译器诊断信息指导修复；(2) 当标识符不存在时，从两个代码库中检索匹配代码段作为映射知识来辅助修复。

Result: 在跨fork和跨branch两种场景下，MIP成功解决的补丁数量均超过最佳基线的两倍。

Conclusion: MIP在自动化补丁迁移领域具有实际应用价值，通过LLM、编译器和代码分析工具的协作，能有效解决隐式不一致问题，并得到行业合作伙伴的用户研究验证。

Abstract: Promptly porting patches from a source codebase to its variants (e.g., forks and branches) is essential for mitigating propagated defects and vulnerabilities. Recent studies have explored automated patch porting to reduce manual effort and delay, but existing approaches mainly handle inconsistencies visible in a patch's local context and struggle with those requiring global mapping knowledge between codebases. We refer to such non-local inconsistencies as implicit inconsistencies. Implicit inconsistencies pose greater challenges for developers to resolve due to their non-local nature. To address them, we propose MIP, which enables collaboration among an LLM, a compiler, and code analysis utilities. MIP adopts different strategies for different cases: when source identifiers exist in the target codebase, it leverages compiler diagnostics; otherwise, it retrieves matched code segment pairs from the two codebases as mapping knowledge for mitigation. Experiments on two representative scenarios, cross-fork and cross-branch patch porting, show that MIP successfully resolves more than twice as many patches as the best-performing baseline in both settings. A user study with our industry partner further demonstrates its practical effectiveness.

</details>


### [143] [Fuzzing REST APIs in Industry: Necessary Features and Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01759)
*Andrea Arcuri,Alexander Poth,Olsi Rrjolli,Philip Garrett,Juan P. Galeotti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 大众汽车将学术原型工具EvoMaster（基于搜索的REST API模糊测试工具）集成到工业环境中，验证了其在实际测试中的价值，并通过用户研究收集了测试专家的反馈，为进一步改进工具以满足工业需求提供了实践经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REST API在工业界广泛应用，但传统测试方法耗时且需要专业人员；学术原型工具（如EvoMaster）虽有研究基础，但缺乏工业集成验证，需要探索如何让这些工具真正落地并产生实际影响。

Method: 大众汽车从2023年至2026年使用EvoMaster进行实际测试，收集了4个API的价值反馈，并联合4家公司的11名测试专家开展用户研究，同时识别了需要解决的工业级研究挑战。

Result: 验证了EvoMaster在工业环境中的实用价值，明确了需要实现的功能特性以支持工业级应用，并识别出多个有待解决的工业实践挑战。

Conclusion: 学术工具向工业应用转化需要针对性改进；EvoMaster具备工业应用潜力，但仍需解决实际挑战才能在工业场景中成功部署。

Abstract: REST APIs are widely used in industry, in all different kinds of domains. An example is Volkswagen AG, a German automobile manufacturer. Established testing approaches for REST APIs are time consuming, and require expertise from professional test engineers. Due to its cost and importance, in the scientific literature several approaches have been proposed to automatically test REST APIs. The open-source, search-based fuzzer EvoMaster is one of such tools proposed in the academic literature. However, how academic prototypes can be integrated in industry and have real impact to software engineering practice requires more investigation. In this paper, we report on our experience in using EvoMaster at Volkswagen AG, as an EvoMaster user from 2023 to 2026.
